### [1] [PsyWorld: Profile-Conditioned World Modeling for Human Behavior Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24999)
*Ziyuan Qin,Yang Qiao,Summer B. Frandsen,Michael T. Treadway,Liang Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种由心理测量画像条件化的状态世界模型，将环境—行为状态转移作为显式监督信号（后验编码器量化真实转移，先验从画像与转移前上下文预测码），用于主体泛化的行为预测，在VEET上达0.904 Top-1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个人化智能体通常直接以静态人格画像为动作条件，未将实际发生的状态转移作为显式监督来源；需要理解稳定心理特质与时变状态如何共同驱动行为。

Method: 训练阶段用后验编码器对真实状态转移做量化编码；先验依据心理测量画像与转移前上下文预测该离散码；先验码与预测的下一状态共同条件化宏观行为与基于模拟器的执行，从而以结构化转移监督替代将人格当作直接动作描述符。

Result: 在VEET上Top-1准确率0.904（95% CI [0.895, 0.913]），Macro KL为0.0033；参与者在训练240/验证25/测试48上按人不交叉划分以评估对未见个体的泛化。消融显示Top-1提升主要来自连续下一状态建模，离散码与先验对齐主要改善Top-3与分布对齐；AgentSociety作为结构不同的合成环境亦用于评估。

Conclusion: 转移信息化的世界模型监督有助于主体泛化的行为预测，但不意味着因果心理机制，也不保证标定的长时程状态动态。

Abstract: Understanding how stable psychometric traits and time-varying states jointly inform behavior is an important problem in computational behavioral science. Existing personalized agents commonly condition actions directly on a static profile, leaving realized state transitions unused as an explicit source of supervision. We introduce a profile-conditioned state world model that uses observed environment--behavior state transitions to learn transition-informed representations for behavioral prediction. During training, a posterior encoder quantizes realized transitions, while a prior predicts the resulting codes from the psychometric profile and pre-transition context. The prior code and predicted next state then condition macro-level behavior and simulator-grounded execution. Thus, the world model provides structured transition supervision instead of treating personality as a direct action descriptor. We evaluate the framework in VEET, a controlled behavioral simulator with human trajectories, and in AgentSociety as a structurally different synthetic environment. A participant-disjoint split of 240 training, 25 validation, and 48 test participants evaluates generalization to unseen people. On VEET, the model reaches 0.904 Top-1 accuracy (95\% CI: [0.895, 0.913]) and 0.0033 Macro KL. Ablations associate continuous next-state modeling with most of the Top-1 gain, while discrete codes and prior alignment mainly improve Top-3 and distributional alignment. These results establish transition-informed world-model supervision as useful for subject-generalized behavior prediction, without implying causal psychological mechanisms or calibrated long-horizon state dynamics.

</details>


### [2] [Watch Where You Linearize: Predicting and Correcting Model-Merging Interference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25000)
*Ido Nitzan Hidekel,Dan Raviv*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出用任务A输出沿任务B任务向量的一阶方向导数预测模型合并中的干扰，但强调必须在"接收模型"(receiver model)处评估才有效；据此冻结接收端局部响应构造对合并系数仿射的替代模型，提出无标签、无反向传播的系数校正方法 LR-Merge，普遍优于现有闭式/等向/稀疏化合并方法，且多任务越多增益越大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并通过将各任务向量加到共享骨架上得到合并模型，但干扰评估通常只在构建并评测候选合并之后进行，导致系数搜索代价高昂。作者希望找到一种廉价、可在合并前预测干扰的信号，以降低搜索成本。

Method: (1) 采用任务A输出沿任务B任务向量的first-order directional derivative预测干扰，并分析其评估位置的影响：在接收模型处可预测邻近合并（按任务、按样本），对多任务完整合并需沿合并路径两次评估；在共享预训练骨架处则近乎正交，LoRA语言模型试点仅部分有效，并通过对照实验将失效归因于沿接收端自身任务向量的位移而非单纯参数距离，该失配在微调最初数十步即出现。(2) 冻结接收端局部响应，得到关于合并系数的仿射替代模型，据此构建 LR-Merge：在无标签探针上通过凸的教师匹配优化系数，无需对合并网络反向传播。

Result: 接收端导数将无变化基线的精度误差减半，并能识别多数预测翻转样本；LR-Merge 在全部测试的闭式、等向与稀疏化基础合并器上均有提升，增益一般随合并任务数增加而增大；若改用预训练响应而非接收端局部响应，基础合并反而退化；LR-Merge 与通过合并网络的精确教师匹配效果相当，后者收敛约慢三倍，等算力预算下表现相近；替代模型建成后可在数秒内回答新的系数与任务子集查询。

Conclusion: 干扰可被一阶方向导数预测，但评估位置是关键：须在接收模型处评估，而非共享骨架。基于接收端局部响应的仿射替代模型提供了高效、无标签、免反向传播的合并系数优化框架 LR-Merge，具备良好通用性与查询效率，突显"接收端局部性"对模型合并分析的重要性。

Abstract: Model merging combines separately fine-tuned models by adding their task vectors to a shared backbone. Interference is typically assessed only after constructing and evaluating a candidate merge, making coefficient search expensive. We show that a first-order directional derivative of task A's outputs along task B's vector can predict interference, but only when evaluated at the appropriate model.
  At the receiver model, this derivative forecasts nearby merges per task and per example, halves the accuracy error of a no-change baseline, and identifies most examples whose predictions flip. For full multi-task merges, a single evaluation is insufficient; two evaluations along the merge path are needed. At the shared pretrained backbone, however, the derivative is nearly orthogonal to realized changes in our vision experiments and only partly predictive in LoRA language-model pilots. Controls link this failure to displacement along the receiver's own task vector, not simply parameter distance; the mismatch appears within the first few dozen fine-tuning steps.
  Freezing receiver-local responses produces a surrogate affine in the merge coefficients. We use it to construct LR-Merge, a label-free correction that optimizes coefficients through convex teacher matching on unlabeled probes, without backpropagation through merged networks. LR-Merge improves all tested closed-form, isotropic, and sparsified base mergers, with gains generally increasing as more tasks are merged. Using pretrained rather than receiver-local responses instead degrades the base merge. LR-Merge matches exact teacher matching through a merged network, which takes roughly three times longer to converge and performs similarly at an equal compute budget. Once built, the surrogate answers new coefficient and task-subset queries in seconds.

</details>


### [3] [Density Feedback Control for Transferability of Generative Sparse Adversarial Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25001)
*Dongsu Song,Jay Hoon Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出密度反馈控制（DFC），按每轮观测硬掩码密度与目标的差距动态调节稀疏系数，使基于生成器的稀疏对抗攻击在单次运行内达到目标密度并提升迁移攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成器式稀疏对抗攻击的扰动密度仅由固定稀疏系数间接控制，该系数到密度的映射未知且随训练漂移，需反复调参才能达到目标密度；且密度下降开始后，固定压力几乎不再带来迁移性收益。

Method: DFC在每个epoch结束时依据观测硬掩码密度与目标密度的差距收紧或放松稀疏系数，不改变生成器架构与损失函数；并在GPG训练中追踪系数变化，以及对EGS-TSSA（限制扰动于源模型特征重要性区域）进行交互分析。

Result: 在10%目标密度下，DFC单次运行即把三种基攻击的平均密度控制在目标±1个百分点内，其中两种攻击在CNN与Transformer目标上迁移ASR提升（含逐图像ℓ0预算情形），且扰动像素更少；GPG中系数放松时迁移ASR随密度回升并在更少像素下反超固定系数基线；EGS-TSSA未获一致增益。

Conclusion: 以密度为反馈量的闭环系数调节可有效解耦"密度可控性"与"迁移性"问题，替代人工调参；但密度反馈的收益依赖扰动空间不受源模型特征重要性区域强约束。

Abstract: Generator-based sparse adversarial attacks train encoder--decoder networks to produce transferable sparse perturbations for black-box robustness evaluation. Their perturbation density, however, is set only indirectly by a fixed sparsity coefficient whose mapping to density is unknown and shifts during training, so reaching a target density requires re-tuning; this fixed pressure also yields little transferability gain once density reduction begins. We propose Density Feedback Control (DFC), which tightens or relaxes this coefficient at the end of each epoch according to the gap between the observed hard-mask density and the target, without changing the generator architecture or loss. At a 10\% target density, DFC brings the mean density of three base attacks within one percentage point of the target in a single run and improves the transfer attack success rate (ASR) of two of them on CNN and Transformer targets, including under per-image $\ell_0$ budgets, while perturbing fewer pixels. Tracing GPG's training, we observe that as DFC relaxes the coefficient, the transfer ASR recovers with the density and overtakes the fixed-coefficient baseline with fewer pixels. For EGS-TSSA, which restricts perturbations to source-model feature-importance regions, DFC yields no consistent gain; we analyze how this restriction interacts with density feedback.

</details>


### [4] [A Model of Diverse Sampling from Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25003)
*Manuel Prada-Corral,Yahya Emara,Timothy J. O'Donnell,Ryan Cotterell,Tim Vieira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PoolDPP 将语言模型的多样性生成形式化为在确定性点过程（DPP）上采样 K 个完整字符串的子集，用有限候选池 + 重要性加权 + K-DPP 采样器实现可处理近似，并证明 KL 散度以 O(1/N) 收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型常能产生高质量的单样本，但整体样本集质量差：重复样本聚集在同一语义模式上。需要一种能同时兼顾个体质量与输出间排斥（去重）的"整集"生成框架。

Method: 将多样性生成建模为从 DPP 中采样大小 K 的完整字符串子集：似然编码质量，嵌入几何编码相似输出间的排斥。因全字符串精确推断不可行，提出 PoolDPP：从可处理提议分布抽取有限候选池，对候选做重要性加权向目标质量校正，再在诱导池核上运行 K-DPP 采样器；并提供贪心 MAP 变体实现确定性选择。

Result: 给出带显式常数的有限样本界：采样器输出与目标 K-DPP 的 KL 散度随池大小 N 以 O(1/N) 衰减。实验上，PoolDPP 在开放式生成中扩展质量—多样性前沿，在歧义 text-to-SQL（Ambrosia）上扩展精确率—召回率前沿，在数学推理（GSM8K）上于大致相同 pass@K 下提升多样性。

Conclusion: PoolDPP 为语言模型的集合级多样性生成提供了有理论保证且实用的 DPP 近似方案，在多个任务上优于现有质量—多样性权衡，验证了"质量似然 + 嵌入排斥"建模范式的有效性。

Abstract: Language models often produce high-quality individual samples but poor sets, with repeated samples clustering around the same semantic modes. We formalize diverse generation as sampling a size-$K$ subset of complete strings from a determinantal point process (DPP), where the likelihood encodes item quality, and the embedding geometry encodes repulsion between similar outputs. Exact inference over all strings is intractable, so we propose PoolDPP: draw a finite candidate pool from a tractable proposal, importance-weight the candidates toward the target quality, and run a $K$-DPP sampler on the induced pool kernel; a greedy MAP variant provides deterministic selection. We prove a finite-sample bound with an explicit constant: the KL divergence between the sampler's output and the target $K$-DPP decays as O(1/N) in the pool size. Empirically, PoolDPP extends the quality--diversity frontier on open-ended generation and the precision--recall frontier on ambiguous text-to-SQL (Ambrosia), and on mathematical reasoning (GSM8K), it raises diversity at about the same pass@$K$.

</details>


### [5] [On the Impact of Feature Reduction Techniques on Adversarial Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25004)
*Mohammad Meymani,Roozbeh Razavi-Far*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究在APT数据集上系统评估PCA、置换重要性排序、递归特征消除、单变量互信息四种特征降维技术对SVM、决策树、随机森林、MLP和KNN在投毒、后门与通用对抗扰动攻击下鲁棒性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特征降维常用于降低维度、缩短训练时间、避免维数灾难、抑制过拟合与冗余特征，但其对不同分类器及不同攻击场景下对抗鲁棒性的影响尚缺乏充分探索。

Method: 以APT数据集为对象，对SVM、决策树、随机森林、多层感知机、K近邻五类模型，分别在投毒攻击、后门攻击和通用对抗扰动攻击下，对比应用PCA、置换重要性排序、递归特征消除、单变量互信息四类特征降维技术前后的表现，采用准确率、F1分数与缓解分数（mitigation score）作为评价指标。

Result: 置换重要性排序与单变量互信息总体上带来最一致的鲁棒性提升；其余方法的效果随分类器与攻击场景不同而波动较大。

Conclusion: 特征降维并非普遍提升对抗鲁棒性，其效果依赖于方法与模型—攻击组合；基于特征重要性/互信息的降维是较为稳健的选择。

Abstract: Adversarial attacks have posed significant threats to machine learning models by exploiting their inherent vulnerabilities. Researchers have established an interdisciplinary field called adversarial machine learning to explore these vulnerabilities and develop corresponding defense strategies. Feature reduction strategies are used to decrease the dimensionality of the data in order to reduce training time, avoid the curse of dimensionality, prevent overfitting, and mitigate redundant features. However, their effect on adversarial robustness across different classifiers and attack scenarios remains insufficiently explored. This study aims to analyze the effect of feature reduction techniques on the adversarial robustness of a diverse set of machine learning models under poisoning, backdoor, and universal adversarial perturbation attacks on the APT dataset. These models include support vector machine, decision tree, random forest, multi-layer perceptron, and k-nearest neighbors. The attained results are compared with scenarios where different feature reduction techniques are applied, including principal component analysis, permutation importance ranking, recursive feature elimination, and univariate mutual information. Metrics such as accuracy, F1-score, and mitigation score are employed to evaluate the effect of these techniques on adversarial robustness. The results demonstrate that permutation importance ranking and univariate mutual information generally provide the most consistent robustness improvements, while other methods exhibit more variable effects depending on the classifier and attack scenario.

</details>


### [6] [Near-Quadratic Sample Compression for All VC Classes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25011)
*Yang Cai,Vineet Gupta,Yanchen Jiang,Christopher Liaw,Aranyak Mehta,Shay Moran,Grigoris Velegkas,Di Wang,Manfred K. Warmuth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了样本压缩的弱猜想：任意VC维为d的二分类概念类都存在大小为O(d²log²d)的样本压缩方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 样本压缩猜想是学习理论中长期未解的核心问题，揭示了"简化与泛化"之间的联系。Floyd与Warmuth（1995）提出强猜想（大小O(d)）与弱猜想（大小poly(d)），二者均长期未决；此前仅Moran与Yehudayoff（2016）给出了仅依赖d但指数级（关于d）的上界。

Method: 通过显式构造一个大小为O(d²log²d)的样本压缩方案，证明弱猜想成立。

Result: 得到大小为O(d²log²d)的样本压缩方案，即多项式于d，从而解决弱样本压缩猜想。

Conclusion: 弱样本压缩猜想被证明成立；强猜想（O(d)）仍然开放，指数上界被改进为多项式上界。

Abstract: The sample compression conjecture is a longstanding open problem in learning theory, originating in the work of Littlestone and Warmuth in the 1980s. At its heart is a connection between simplification and generalization: if a predictor consistent with a training sample is defined using only a small part of that sample, then it is likely to generalize. Sample compression schemes formalize this idea, and many natural learning algorithms can be analyzed this way.
  The sample compression conjecture of Floyd and Warmuth (1995) asserts that every binary concept class of VC dimension $d$ admits a sample compression scheme of size $O(d)$; a weaker conjecture asks only for size polynomial in $d$. Both have remained open: the first bound depending only on $d$, due to Moran and Yehudayoff (2016), is exponential in $d$. We prove the weaker conjecture by constructing a sample compression scheme of size $O(d^2 \log^2 d)$.

</details>


### [7] [HapBO: Harnessed $α$-$π$ Bayesian Optimization via Expert Natural Language Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25100)
*Jean-Sébastien Giroux,Rose Guay Hottin,Marco Bonizzato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HapBO 将自然语言先验一次性编译为可复用的信念函数，通过有界正乘子加权任意非负采集函数，实现零在线 LLM 调用的先验引导贝叶斯优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先验知识能显著提升 BO 样本效率，但实践中先验多以自然语言形式存在（专家直觉、文档），而非先验引导 BO 所需的显式概率分布景观；同时需具备从语言到先验的表达能力，并对可能误导的信息保持鲁棒。

Method: 提出 HapBO（Harnessed α-π Bayesian Optimization）：由人类专家、LLM 或两者一次性引出信念，确定性地编译为结构化规范并生成可复用的信念函数 π: X→[0,1]，表示形式不限于高斯族；在 α-πBO 基础上，以有界正乘子作用于任意非负采集函数，保持正采集支撑，优化过程中无需 LLM 调用。理论上证明对 EI，即便信念为对抗性，HapBO 仍以有界因子保持标准简单遗憾率。

Result: 在 40 个 HPOBench 任务、6–15 维合成测试集及皮层神经刺激、受限太阳能设计两个真实应用上评估：HapBO 取得最低的聚合 HPOBench 遗憾，真实任务表现强，在线 token 成本为零；在 12–20 次查询的小预算下即改善优化结果，且墙钟时间与 token 成本仅为其他 LLM 类方法的一小部分。

Conclusion: 将自然语言先验一次性编译为信念函数并与采集函数有界耦合，可在不牺牲理论保证、无在线 LLM 开销的前提下高效利用先验，适用于低预算真实场景。

Abstract: Bayesian Optimization (BO) can be substantially more sample-efficient when informed by prior knowledge about the problem. In practice, however, such knowledge is often available only in natural language, e.g., expert intuitions or documentation, rather than as the explicit probability distribution landscape that prior-guided BO methods may require. Leveraging it effectively demands both an expressive mapping from language to priors and robustness to information that may be misleading. We propose HapBO (Harnessed $α$-$π$ Bayesian Optimization), which elicits beliefs once from a human expert, a large language model (LLM), or both, and deterministically compiles a structured specification into a reusable belief function ($π: \mathcal{X} \to[0,1]$), with a rich representation not restricted to a Gaussian family. Building on $α$-$π$BO, HapBO applies this belief function through a bounded positive multiplier on any nonnegative acquisition, preserving positive acquisition support and requiring no LLM calls during optimization. We prove that, for the Expected Improvement acquisition function (EI), HapBO preserves the standard simple-regret rate up to a bounded factor, even under adversarial beliefs. We evaluate HapBO on 40 HPOBench tasks, a synthetic suite in 6-15 dimensions and two real-world applications: cortical neurostimulation and constrained solar design. HapBO achieves the lowest aggregate HPOBench regret and strong real-world task performance, with zero in-loop token cost. In real-world applications it improves the optimization outcome even at budgets of 12-20 queries, and at a small fraction of the wall-clock and token costs of other LLM-based methods.

</details>


### [8] [Long-Range Video Presentation Attack Detection: A Three-Class Benchmark Across 1-100 m and Two Sensor Types](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25108)
*Lambert Igene,Mohammad Zahir Uddin Chowdhury,Afzal Hossain,Sandip Purnapatra,Peter Johnson,Stephanie Schuckers*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向远距离（1–100 m）视频人脸呈现攻击检测数据集（261 个身份、37.4 万+ 帧、室内外、单反与手机双传感器、活体/欺骗/遮挡道具三分类），并基准测试四种 CNN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人脸 PAD 数据与模型均假设近距离（<1 m）拍摄，缺乏同时覆盖多距离、多环境条件以及欺骗与遮挡攻击并存的系统性基准，导致实际部署于边境管控、机场监控的模型准备不足。

Method: 构建基于视频的 PAD 数据集：261 个身份、37.4 万+ 帧，5 档拍摄距离（1–100 m），室内外条件，DSLR（C）与移动传感器（M）采集，三分类标签（live、spoof、props 遮挡）；以视频片段而非单帧进行训练与评估；微调并基准测试 MobileNetV3、MobileNet、EfficientNet、ResNet-50 四种 CNN。

Result: 高分辨率 DSLR 在各距离下欺骗分类率近乎完美；低分辨率移动传感器在 50 m 后显著退化，MobileNetV3 在 75 m 处 props 分类率降至 55%。EfficientNet 综合最佳（98.48% 准确率，ACER 2.01%）。在未见攻击数据集上泛化测试达 89–94% 准确率，验证跨域鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 拍摄距离与传感器分辨率是 PAD 难度中关键但未被充分探索的维度；数据集将在最终用户许可协议下公开，以支持可复现的远距离 PAD 研究。

Abstract: Face presentation attack detection (PAD) systems deployed in border control and airport surveillance are constrained by a critical assumption: subjects are captured at close range, typically under one meter. No existing benchmark systematically addresses multi-distance capture, diverse environmental conditions, and concurrent spoofing and occlusion-based attacks, leaving deployed models underprepared for real-world scenarios. We introduce a video-based PAD dataset of 374,000+ frames from 261 unique identities collected across five standoff distances (1 m-100 m) under indoor and outdoor conditions using a DSLR camera (C) and a mobile sensor (M), with three-class labels: live, spoof, and props (occlusions); models are trained and evaluated on video clips rather than single frames. We fine-tune and benchmark four CNN architectures: MobileNetV3, MobileNet, EfficientNet, and ResNet-50. The higher-resolution DSLR maintains near-perfect spoof classification rate across all distances, while the lower-resolution mobile sensor degrades sharply beyond 50 m, with props classification rate dropping to 55% at 75 m for MobileNetV3. EfficientNet achieves the best overall performance (98.48% accuracy, ACER 2.01%). Generalization tests on unseen attack datasets yield 89-94% accuracy, validating cross-domain robustness. These results establish capture distance and sensor resolution as critical but underexplored axes of PAD difficulty. The dataset will be publicly released under an End-User License Agreement to support reproducible long-range PAD research.

</details>


### [9] [Fast and Memory-Light Evaluation of Density-Based Clusterings with the DISCO Index in R](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25114)
*Amin Entezari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文介绍R包discoCVI 0.2.0版本的重构实现：将DISCO聚类有效性指数的全部计算量归约到互可达最小生成树的单链接合并树上，使内存从O(n²)降为O(n)，时间复杂度仍为O(n²)，并修正了单点簇的评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DISCO 是近期提出的内部聚类有效性指数，可评价基于密度聚类（如HDBSCAN）的噪声标签质量，但按定义需计算所有点对间的密度连通距离，直接实现需存储多个 n×n 矩阵，内存开销巨大，限制了其在大规模数据上的可用性。

Method: 从理论上证明 DISCO 所需的每一项量均可从互可达最小生成树（mutual-reachability MST）的单链接合并树中读出；据此在 discoCVI 0.2.0 中实现精确、线性内存的算法，运行时间保持二次；并与旧版本及原始 Python 实现做一致性测试和基准测试。

Result: 与原始 Python 实现误差小于 3×10⁻¹³；单点簇得分修正为符合发表定义；500–4000 点时较旧版快 23–138 倍，500–8000 点时较 Python 参考实现快 3–9 倍，内存占用均显著更低；10 万点约 50 秒完成，占用不到 200 MB。

Conclusion: 基于合并树的归约使 DISCO 可在 R 中高精度、低内存地计算，支持无真值时比较聚类结果与调优 HDBSCAN 参数，适用于大规模数据。

Abstract: DISCO is a recent internal cluster validity index for density-based clustering that, unlike earlier indices, also evaluates the quality of noise labels. The discoCVI package makes DISCO available in R. This article describes the design of the package and a typical workflow, in which DISCO is used to compare clusterings and to choose the parameters of HDBSCAN without ground truth, followed by the implementation introduced in version 0.2.0. DISCO is defined through density-connectivity distances between all pairs of points, so direct implementations store several n-by-n matrices. We show that every quantity DISCO needs can be read off the single-linkage merge tree of the mutual-reachability minimum spanning tree, so that the index can be computed exactly with memory that grows linearly with the number of points, while the running time remains quadratic as in the original definition. The implementation is tested against the previous version, agrees with the original Python implementation to within $3 \times 10^{-13}$, and corrects the score of singleton clusters to match the published definition. In benchmarks, discoCVI is 23 to 138 times faster than its previous version for 500 to 4,000 points and 3 to 9 times faster than the Python reference for 500 to 8,000 points, using a fraction of the memory of both, and it scores 100,000 points in about 50 seconds with less than 200 MB of memory.

</details>


### [10] [Auditing Learned Dynamics in Weather Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25119)
*Skylar Gu,Christina Last*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 利用GraphCast的稀疏自编码器特征检验"因果相关性是否需要物理语义"，并展示物理相关隐方向可被操纵以改进极端事件预报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习型天气模型是内部表征可被检查与干预的实验性计算系统；在高保障场景中审计AI预报员是否依赖可靠机制，需要厘清"可解释/物理语义"与"因果效用"是否等同。同时探索能否通过干预内部激活改善极端事件预报。

Method: 从GraphCast（DeepMind自回归天气预报模型）中提取稀疏自编码器特征；通过消融/抑制/放缩特征进行因果干预，干预施加于rollout过程中的模型内部激活；检验与计算网格锁定的特征，以及对流和低层涡度相关特征的影响。

Result: 消融一个锁定于GraphCast计算网格、缺乏常规物理语义的特征，仍会增大全球位势高度误差；抑制对流与低层涡度相关特征会削弱预测的热带气旋加深（中心气压下降），而放大它们可降低强度误差——通过特征缩放放大对流特征，在七个风暴上实现中位数51%的误差降低。

Conclusion: 可将"可解释的物理关联"与"因果效用"区分开来；稀疏特征可作为审计与干预学习型天气动力学的实验坐标。

Abstract: Learned weather models are experimentally accessible computational systems whose internal representations can be inspected and manipulated. Using sparse autoencoder features from GraphCast, DeepMind's autoregressive weather forecasting model, we test whether causal relevance requires physical semantics, a question that matters for auditing whether AI forecasters used in high-assurance settings rely on reliable mechanisms. We also look at whether physically associated latent directions can be steered to improve extreme-event forecasts, where interventions are applied to the model's internal activations during the rollout. We find that ablating a feature locked to GraphCast's computational mesh increases global geopotential height error despite lacking conventional physical semantics. Second, suppressing convection- and low-level-vorticity-associated features weakens predicted tropical-cyclone deepening (the fall in central pressure during intensification), while amplifying them reduces intensity error, with amplification of convection features through feature scaling achieving a median of 51\% error reduction over seven storms. Together, these results separate interpretable, physical association from causal utility and establish sparse features as experimental coordinates for auditing and intervening on learned weather dynamics.

</details>


### [11] [Temporal Extrapolation of Elastic Seismic Wave Propagation in Layered Media Using Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25130)
*Dibyajyoti Nayak,Yuze Pu,Kami Mohammadi,Somdatta Goswami*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TI-DeepONet与FNO耦合的预测-校正神经算子框架，在参考应力条件下实现分层弹性介质速度场的自回归时间外推预测，NRMSE约0.53%–2.31%（外推区间0.61%–2.82%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子模型难以对训练时间区间之外的地震速度场进行时间外推预测，需在应力条件约束下实现跨区间的时间推演。

Method: 构建耦合神经算子：TI-DeepONet内嵌四阶Runge-Kutta积分推进速度场，FNO学习对预测空间场的残差修正，二者以预测-校正方式联合训练；以前两个时间层参考速度场初始化，自回归外推，全程（含训练区间之外）输入参考应力场与指定激励。算例为50 m与280 m总深四层土层中的SH波，DOG3子波激励，中心频率0.5–6 Hz，层厚与剪切波速随剖面变化。

Result: 预测能再现主要速度到时与深度相关响应特征；四个算例全评估窗NRMSE为0.53%–2.31%，外推区间（最长为训练时长2倍）为0.61%–2.82%，归一化采用全评估窗内各剖面参考速度峰值绝对值；精度随剖面而异。

Conclusion: 结果验证了在参考应力条件下进行时间速度预测的可行性，但速度-应力波场的自主耦合演化尚未实现，仍依赖外部持续提供的参考应力与激励。

Abstract: Temporal prediction of seismic velocity fields beyond the time interval represented in training remains challenging for neural operator models. This study develops a coupled neural operator framework for stress-conditioned temporal prediction in layered elastic media. A time-integrator-embedded Deep Operator Network (TI-DeepONet) advances the velocity field through an embedded fourth-order Runge-Kutta (RK4) scheme, while a Fourier Neural Operator (FNO) learns a residual correction to the predicted spatial field. The two components are trained jointly in a predictor-corrector configuration. Prediction is initialized with reference velocity fields at the first two time levels and proceeds autoregressively, with reference stress fields and prescribed excitation supplied throughout rollout, including beyond the training interval. Four cases consider horizontally polarized shear (SH) waves in four-layer soil columns with fixed total depths of 50 and 280 m, excited by third-order Gaussian-derivative (DOG3) wavelets with central frequencies between 0.5 and 6 Hz. Layer thicknesses and shear-wave velocities vary across profiles. Evaluations on test profiles extend beyond the training interval by up to twice its duration. The predictions reproduce principal velocity arrivals and depth-dependent response patterns, with accuracy varying across profiles. Across the four cases, mean peak-normalized root-mean-square errors (NRMSEs) range from 0.53% to 2.31% over the full evaluation windows and from 0.61% to 2.82% over the extrapolation intervals. Both metrics use each profile's peak absolute reference velocity over the full evaluation window as the normalizer. These results demonstrate temporal velocity prediction under reference-stress conditioning for the tested configurations; autonomous continuation of the coupled velocity-stress wavefield remains to be established.

</details>


### [12] [Rate-distortion Assessment for Hierarchical Content Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25151)
*Yunye Gong,Indranil Sur,Jared Claypoole,Pritish Sahu,Ajay Divakaran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文将层次化内容摘要形式化为信息编码问题，借助率失真理论构建语义层次框架，定量刻画生成式内容操作中压缩效率与信息损失的权衡，并用于大模型基准评测与检索增强生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式模型在内容生成、编辑与操作任务中表现出色，但对其生成内容质量的评估仍以主观判断为主，缺乏对模型能力的科学量化理解。

Method: 受Shannon信息压缩理论中的率失真理论启发，将层次化内容摘要建模为信息编码问题；设计原则性框架提取源内容的语义层次结构，以定量刻画压缩效率（rate）与信息损失（distortion）之间的率失真权衡，从而在给定速率预算下对各类模型进行基准测试；并将语义层次用作检索增强生成的引导信号。

Result: 在长上下文问答的内容摘要任务上，利用最先进大语言模型开展实证实验，获得关于其效率与理解能力的新洞见；同时以语义层次引导检索增强生成，相较现有基线展现出更优的率失真特性。

Conclusion: 该框架为生成式内容操作提供了客观、可量化的评估范式，将主观质量评价转化为可比较的率失真分析，并可作为提升检索增强生成效率的实用指导。

Abstract: Powerful generative models including large language and vision models have demonstrated success across a wide range of content generation, editing and manipulation tasks via end-to-end prompt-based generation. However, current assessment of the quality of generative content manipulation remains subjective, limiting scientific understanding of model capabilities. Inspired by rate-distortion theory from Shannon information compression theory, in this work we formalize hierarchical content summarization as an information encoding problem. We design a principled framework to extract the semantic hierarchy of the source content to support quantitative characterization of the rate-distortion trade-off between compression efficiency (rate) against information loss (distortion) during generative content manipulation, which allows us to benchmark various models with respect to performance conditional on rate budget of interest. We demonstrate the use of the framework with empirical experiments on content summarization for long-context question-answering with state-of-the-art large language models, offering new insights into their efficiency and comprehension capabilities. We further demonstrate the use of the semantic hierarchy as guidance for performing effective retrieval augmented generation, showing improved rate-distortion characteristics against current baselines.

</details>


### [13] [Bayesian $κ$-Explorer for Minimax Model Estimation in MDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25160)
*Xihe Gu,Urbashi Mitra,Tara Javidi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Bayesian κ-Explorer：在转移分布上放置Dirichlet先验，以总后验方差作为闭式探索信号，在后验均值模型下规划，避免置信集优化，保持Õ(T^{-1/3})代理收敛率，实验中最坏情形转移估计误差更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未知MDP中minimax转移估计的主动探索问题。现有frequentist κ-Explorer需构造置信界并在可行转移核与占用测度上联合优化，计算负担重、实用性受限。

Method: 对转移分布施加Dirichlet先验；利用后验均值估计下“后验期望平方ℓ2误差=总后验方差”的性质得到闭式探索信号；算法在后验均值模型下进行规划，无需置信集优化；并给出最大后验方差相对最优渐近minimax分配基准的界。

Result: 在给定假设下保持frequentist κ-Explorer的Õ(T^{-1/3})代理收敛率；给出最大后验方差相对最优渐近minimax分配基准的上界；在Pendulum、Mountain Car、Taxi上worst-case转移估计误差低于对比基线。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯化可显著简化minimax转移估计探索的计算流程而不牺牲理论速率，为实用主动探索提供更高效方案。

Abstract: We study active exploration for minimax transition estimation in unknown Markov decision processes (MDPs). Frequentist $κ$-Explorer~\citep{gu2026kappa} addresses this problem through a parameterized family of exploration objectives, but requires confidence-bound constructions and joint optimization over plausible transition kernels and occupancy measures, introducing a computational burden that limits its practical applicability. We develop Bayesian $κ$-Explorer, which simplifies this procedure by placing Dirichlet priors on the transition distributions. Under the posterior mean estimator, the posterior expected squared $\ell_2$ error equals the total posterior variance, providing a closed-form exploration signal. The algorithm plans under the posterior mean model, eliminating confidence-set optimization. Under the stated assumptions, this simpler planning procedure retains the $\widetilde{O}(T^{-1/3})$ surrogate convergence rate of frequentist $κ$-Explorer. We further bound the maximum posterior variance relative to the optimal asymptotic minimax allocation benchmark. Experiments on Pendulum, Mountain Car, and Taxi demonstrate lower worst-case transition-estimation errors than the tested baselines.

</details>


### [14] [Separating Interpretability and Reconstruction in Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25165)
*Anh Tien Nguyen,Dimitris Samaras,Mahdi S. Hosseini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 DualSAE：在标准 SAE 上增加可插拔 Residual Branch，将稀疏编码（主导语义）与非语义残差分离，用去相关损失减少两分支重叠并配两阶段训练；在两组视觉数据与两组语言语料上，重建保真度平均相对提升约29%、单义性可解释性提升约34%，并在主题建模、假设生成与 LLM steering 中取得一致增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 SAE 用单一稀疏表示同时承担重建保真度与可解释性两个目标，而二者并不必然一致，导致目标耦合冲突。

Method: 模块化框架：标准 SAE 作为 Sparse Branch 学习输入主导语义的稀疏码；新增插件的 Residual Branch 建模被稀疏分支遗漏的非语义信息；通过 decorrelation loss 使两分支对整体重建的贡献重叠最小化；采用两阶段训练，先稳定稀疏表示学习，再与残差分支耦合。

Result: 在多种 encoder-SAE 配置、两个视觉数据集和两个语言语料上，重建保真度平均相对提升约29%，可解释性（monosemanticity score）平均相对提升约34%；在下游主题建模、假设生成和 LLM steering 上均获得一致性能增益。定性分析显示 Sparse Branch 保留主导语义，Residual Branch 捕获非语义信息以提升重建保真度。

Conclusion: 通过将重建保真度与可解释性拆分为两个专门分支并显式去相关，DualSAE 可同时提升稀疏表示的重建质量与单义性，并在下游任务中带来实际收益，为机制可解释性提供更模块化的 SAE 设计。

Abstract: Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) are effective tools for mechanistic interpretability by decomposing neural representations into sparse and interpretable features. However, a single sparse representation in traditional SAEs is responsible for both reconstruction fidelity and interpretability, which are not guaranteed to align. To separate these objectives, we propose DualSAE, a modular framework that augments standard SAEs with a plug-in Residual Branch. A standard SAE serves as the Sparse Branch, learning a sparse code that contains the dominant semantics of the input. The added Residual Branch models the non-semantic information omitted by the Sparse Branch. We ensure that the contributions of the two branches towards the overall reconstruction have minimal overlap through a decorrelation loss. We also adopt a two-stage training strategy that first stabilizes sparse representation learning before coupling with the Residual Branch. Across diverse encoder-SAE configurations on two vision datasets and two language corpora, DualSAE achieves mean relative improvements of approximately 29% in reconstruction fidelity and 34% in interpretability (measured by monosemanticity score). Moreover, with more interpretable representations, DualSAE yields consistent performance gains in the downstream applications of topic modeling, hypothesis generation, and LLM steering. Qualitative analysis further suggests that the Sparse Branch indeed preserves dominant semantic content, whereas the Residual Branch captures non-semantic information to improve reconstruction fidelity.

</details>


### [15] [Game-Theoretic Data Allocation for Expert Specialization and Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25167)
*Mark Lovett,Denis Lim,Soroush Vosoughi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 InflEns，一种混合 LoRA 专家框架，其训练权重与推理路由来自固定 prompt 特征空间中的同一位置博弈，使专家分工在训练前可检查，并可据此定位于单个专家以修复有害行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者关心的问题是：用于训练专家的分配（allocation）能否同时预测哪个专家在某 prompt 上表现最好。若成立，则专家分工可在训练前被审视，且分配本身可指导推理路由，无需单独训练门控网络。

Method: 在固定的 prompt-trait 空间中构造位置博弈：玩家（专家）选择位置以最大化其在 prompt 分布上的期望份额，且不使用专家损失。利用该博弈的 kernel-general 理论判据判断共位位置何时对分离局部不稳定。以该博弈分配同时作为 LoRA 专家训练权重来源与推理路由，构成 InflEns；并在七个安全相关基准构建的语料上评估。

Result: 在 sharpened simplex 表示下，四种 kernel 中最高份额专家在约 90% 的 prompt 上持有最低的留出响应 NLL，而将分配在 prompt 间置换后仅约 15%。性能与已发表的学习式门控相当，且无需显式训练门控。因路由固定，可将有害行为追溯至单个专家并仅重训该专家，使 HarmBench 攻击成功率从 0.51 降至 0.02，同时保持指令遵循能力。

Conclusion: 训练用分配可有效预测推理路由，InflEns 提供可检查、可追溯的专家分工机制，并可通过 kernel 选择及其参数、特征空间几何、专家数量等控制专家专业化；该框架可推广至任意编码器与数据分布。

Abstract: Can the allocation used to train experts also predict which expert will perform best on a prompt? Such a connection would make the intended division of labor inspectable before training and allow the allocation itself to guide inference. We introduce InflEns, a mixture of LoRA experts whose training weights and inference routes come from the same location game in a fixed prompt-trait space. Players choose positions to maximize their expected shares of the prompt distribution, without using expert losses. We utilize the game's kernel-general theoretical criterion for when co-located positions become locally unstable to separation. We evaluate InflEns on a corpus assembled from seven safety-related benchmarks, which demonstrates that the highest-share expert has the lowest held-out response negative log-likelihood (NLL) on approximately 90% of prompts across four kernels under a sharpened simplex representation, compared with approximately 15% after permuting allocations across prompts. We demonstrate that the method performs comparably to published learned gates without having to train an explicit gate on the experts. Because the router is fixed, we can trace harmful behavior to a single expert and retrain only that expert, which lowers HarmBench attack success from 0.51 to 0.02 while keeping instruction following. The game also gives us several controls over how the experts specialize, including the kernel choice and its parameters, the trait-space geometry, and the number of experts. Importantly, InflEns is a general framework that can be extended to any encoder and data distribution.

</details>


### [16] [PDE-State Conditioning for Learned Optimization Using Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25185)
*Farzana Yasmin,Ricardo Vilalta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种以PDE级状态信息为条件的元学习优化器：构造11维PDE状态（算子结构、残差几何、短时程更新响应），通过有界FiLM调制学习到的更新规则；实验确定紧凑残差几何状态G2与有界FiLM为最优组合，在Linear Advection、KdV、Burgers上全面优于Adam和无条件学习优化器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINN优化依赖底层PDE的全局性质及物理残差在全域的演化，但标准Adam使用固定的梯度统计，已有的学习型PINN优化器仅基于局部参数与优化历史特征构造更新，均未显式利用PDE级信息。作者因此研究两个耦合的设计问题：学习优化器应观测何种PDE级信息，以及该信息应如何影响其更新。

Method: 提出11维PDE状态，涵盖三方面：状态捕获算子结构、残差几何、短时程更新响应；以该状态通过有界特征级调制（bounded FiLM）对结构化元学习优化器进行条件化。通过重复设计研究对比不同状态表示与调制方式。

Result: 在设计研究中，紧凑的残差几何状态G2与有界FiLM在测试方案中综合表现最佳。所得PDE-State条件化优化器在Linear Advection、KdV与Burgers上，对relative-L2、L1、解的MSE与物理MSE全部评价指标均优于Adam及无条件学习优化器。

Conclusion: 显式引入PDE级状态并以有界方式调制学习优化器的更新，可有效提升PINN训练效果；紧凑残差几何信息是其中最具价值的状态成分，有界调制保证了条件化的稳定增益。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural network (PINN) optimization depends on global properties of the underlying PDE and how the physics residual evolves across the domain. Standard optimizers such as Adam use fixed gradient-based statistics, while learned PINN optimizers replace the fixed rule with a learned update built from local parameters and optimization-history features. However, none explicitly use PDE-level information. We study two coupled design questions: what PDE-level information should a learned optimizer observe, and how should that information influence its update? We introduce an 11-dimensional PDE state-capturing operator structure, residual geometry, and short-horizon update response, and use it to condition a structured meta-learned optimizer through bounded feature-wise modulation. Repeated design studies identify the compact residual-geometry state G2 and bounded FiLM as the strongest overall choices among those tested. The resulting PDE-State-conditioned optimizer improves all evaluation metrics (relative-L2, L1, solution MSE, and physics MSE) over Adam and the unconditioned learned optimizer on Linear Advection, KdV, and Burgers.

</details>


### [17] [φTD: Distributional Reinforcement Learning using Characteristic Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25208)
*Thomas Mousseau,Tyler Kastner,Davide Baldelli,Amir-massoud Farahmand*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 φTD，通过在频域计算损失来突破分布式强化学习仅能刻画有限类分布的局限，统一了 quantile 与 categorical 方法，并可学习此前无法表示的分布（如连续分布混合）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式强化学习方法（quantile、categorical）只能覆盖狭窄的一类分布，难以表示连续分布混合等复杂回报分布，表达能力受限。

Method: 将损失计算从值域/分位数域转移到频域（频率域），以频域表示刻画回报分布并据此定义 TD 损失，从而统一 quantile 与 categorical 两类方法。

Result: 在合成基准与大尺度深度强化学习环境中实证评估，表明来自更宽参数族的灵活性可带来性能提升。

Conclusion: 频域建模是提升分布式强化学习分布表示能力的有效途径，φTD 兼具理论统一性与实践优势。

Abstract: Distributional Reinforcement Learning methods aim to learn the entire distribution of returns, yet current algorithmic approaches are limited to a narrow class of distributions. We propose $\varphi$TD, a method to overcome this limitation by computing the loss in the frequency domain. We demonstrate that this further unifies quantile and categorical approaches to distributional RL, and enables the learning of distributions that were previously impossible to capture, such as mixtures of continuous distributions. We empirically study $\varphi$TD, across synthetic benchmarks and large-scale deep RL environments and demonstrate that the flexibility obtained from wider parametric families can lead to improved performance.

</details>


### [18] [Learning to Read Events for Exact State Tracking with Frozen Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25211)
*Jeonghoon Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"冻结语言模型选择器 + 外部整数寄存器"框架用于长事件流的有序状态更新，在置换乘积任务上突破固定深度Transformer的理论与实测局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长事件流需逐条识别变更并按序施加，而固定深度、对数精度Transformer在标准猜想TC⁰≠NC¹下无法对所有输入长度求解五元素置换乘积；同时API-only场景缺乏可靠的状态累积机制。

Method: 在冻结语言模型上训练选择器逐条读取转移；仅API访问时令模型列出转移，由外部整数寄存器按序复合；引入置信度规则标记不确定更新或保留多候选；并采用并发抽取降低时延。对照：纯推理（含/不含中间步骤）、单正则+执行器、频率基线。

Result: 27B模型在两事件窗口无推理3/100、有中间步骤96/100；冻结0.8B+选择器在299/299窗口（各43,687事件）全对，新机器298/299（未达0.99单侧95%下界），后续扩容472/473；1,000事件房间交换文档中纯推理仅3/10模型全对，而抽取+寄存器7/7全对（无需CoT）；并发抽取中位时延Sonnet 5.5由136.1s降至10.4s、GPT-6.1 Sol由313.5s降至28.0s；固定单价下每文档均成本降55%与44%；正则+执行器15/15；自由文本500条评论中4B读者68.8% vs 频率基线61.0%。

Conclusion: 将"识别"与"有序复合"解耦——语言模型负责局部转移抽取、外部寄存器负责精确累积——可突破纯推理的长度-深度瓶颈，同时提升准确率、时延与成本，并支持置信度控制。

Abstract: Long event streams require identifying each change and applying the changes in order. We train a selector on a frozen language model to read each transition. With API-only access, we ask the model to list the transitions. An external integer register composes them in order. A confidence rule can flag uncertain updates or retain several candidates. We study products of permutations of five items, a task that fixed-depth, log-precision transformers cannot solve on all input lengths under the standard conjecture that $\mathrm{TC}^0 \ne \mathrm{NC}^1$. On two-event windows, a 27B model answered 3 of 100 correctly without visible reasoning and 96 of 100 when it generated intermediate steps. A frozen 0.8B model with a trained selector read every event in 299 of 299 windows of 43,687 events each. The same package passed 298 of 299 new windows on a fresh machine, missing the target of 0.99 for the one-sided 95% lower confidence bound. A separate follow-up sized to allow one failure yielded 472 of 473. On 1,000-event room-swap documents, three of ten models solved all five documents by reasoning alone. All seven models tested both ways at this length solved all five with extraction and the register, with no chain of thought requested. With concurrent extraction, median wall-clock time fell from 136.1 to 10.4 s for Sonnet 5.5 and from 313.5 to 28.0 s for GPT-6.1 Sol. At fixed per-token rates, mean cost per document fell by 55% for Sonnet 5.5 and 44% for GPT-6.1 Sol. A regular expression with an executor also solved all 15 fixed-format documents. On 500 free-form issue-tracker comments, a separately adapted 4B reader identified the recorded value in 68.8% of cases, compared with 61.0% for a frequency baseline fitted on the same training projects.

</details>


### [19] [The Window Lever: Longer Observation Windows Outweigh the Observed Effects of Data, Capacity, and Architecture in Customer Behavior Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26521)
*Tara Sowrirajan,Yanqiao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在长期客户行为预测中，更长观测窗口的影响远大于更多数据、更大容量和更新架构；作者报告稳健的正相关，但不识别与日历无关的历史效应幅度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作多聚焦增加数据量、提升模型容量或采用新架构，而忽略了"观测窗口长度"这一输入设计维度；作者希望通过受控实验比较窗口与其他三个杠杆的相对影响力。

Method: 定义窗口为预测周之前的数周历史（8周 vs 16周）；在内部数据与五个公开数据集（其中四个跨一年以上）上，用ridge与自研transformer做对比；采用匹配测试队列（同客户-周）控制样本构成；用周度ridge探针与活动混合模型做预注册盲预测检验；并考察训练期锚点以剥离节假日覆盖的混淆。

Result: 8→16周窗口在1M规模下每个家族族系带来+0.070~0.107的log空间R²原始增益，匹配队列显示原始比较低估该增益；方向在五个公开数据集上一致。更长窗口使训练客户增多最高10%且更"冷"，匹配无法修正。四类杠杆（数据量、容量、架构）效应约小一个数量级：1M→10M最多0.006，五类序列家族间0.0052，容量三种子均值0.006。四个预注册预测中唯一完全盲测者失败（预测+0.014，实测-0.0004）。

Conclusion: 窗口长度与其他杠杆存在约一个数量级的对比，应作为此类数据建模的首选起点；但窗口长度与日历覆盖未完全解耦（所有测试锚点位于同一八周带），故仅主张稳健正相关而非日历无关的效应量级。所报告数值为观测区间而非等价性界。

Abstract: In our experiments on long-horizon customer-behavior forecasting, a longer observation window outweighed the observed effects of more data, more capacity and newer architectures. The window is the weeks of history a model sees before the week it forecasts from. Going from an 8- to a 16-week window adds a raw +0.070 to 0.107 log-space $R^2$ in every family at 1M. Matched test cohorts, which score the same customer-weeks at every window, show this raw comparison understates the gain in every family they cover. The direction holds on five public datasets, four spanning over a year (ridge and our transformer on all five). On our internal data, longer windows admit up to 10% more and colder training customers, which matching does not fix. Our design does not separate window length from calendar coverage, because every test anchor (the week a forecast is made from) falls in one eight-week band. On training-period anchors, the weekly ridge probe's gain persists outside the holiday season, keeping about 72% of it. We therefore report a robust positive association with longer windows and do not identify the magnitude of a calendar-independent history effect. Of four internally registered predictions, the only fully blind one failed. An activity-mixture model predicted +0.014 from 24 to 32 weeks; the weekly-aggregate probe returned -0.0004. For the sequence families, the other three levers are about an order of magnitude smaller, bounded and unresolved. Their observed effects are at most 0.006 between the nominal 1M and 10M tiers and 0.0052 among five sequence families at 16 weeks with clocks as built, both single-seed on the full test split, and 0.006 for capacity on three-seed means. These are observed intervals, not equivalence bounds. Our claim is the order-of-magnitude contrast between the window and those bounds. A team building on this class of data should start by lengthening the window.

</details>


### [20] [Some Memories Must Fade: Persistence and Decay of User Memories in Model Parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25235)
*Sanidhya Vijayvargiya,Rahul Lokesh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种参数化记忆管理系统，由固定容量的k-hot LoRA子层（支持独立时间衰减与抗灾难性干扰）与轻量学习型控制器组成，实现对用户知识生命周期的管理，在长时程用户轨迹数据集上达到oracle 86%的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数化记忆方法大多沿袭持续学习（CL）的"无限期保留"目标，既不可持续也不足以构成真正的记忆系统。记忆系统需在整个生命周期内治理知识：保留稳定事实、强化重复观察、更新变化信念、允许瞬态信息淡出。同时RAG与CL基线通常只偏向召回或弃答之一，无法平衡。

Method: 系统分为两部分：（1）记忆基底——固定容量LoRA适配器，由k-hot秩组合（k-hot rank combinations）构成，实现无灾难性干扰的事实存储，并为每个事实维护独立可控的时间衰减；（2）学习型策略——在上述基底上训练轻量控制器，对每个到来的用户事件决定记什么、记多深、记多久，使记忆状态反映用户真实、更新后的信念。此外构建受控数据集：长时程用户轨迹，事实持久性随信念状态变化与用户行为漂移而变，并配探测查询，在信息有效期内测召回、过期后测弃答。

Result: 策略能在整个生命周期内有效管理用户记忆，平衡召回与弃答，达到oracle性能的86%；而CL与RAG基线大多退化为两种行为之一。

Conclusion: 本文提供了首个直接在模型权重中管理记忆的系统，为长时程参数化记忆奠定基础。

Abstract: Parametric memory allows Large Language Models to internalize knowledge directly into their weights, offering a potentially powerful alternative to retrieval-based memory (RAG). However, existing approaches to parametric memory are difficult to deploy as they largely mirror the Continual Learning (CL) objective of retaining information indefinitely. This is neither sustainable nor sufficient for a memory system, which must govern knowledge across its lifecycle by retaining stable facts, reinforcing recurring observations, updating changed beliefs, and allowing transient information to fade. We propose a system for parametric memory management consisting of a memory substrate (handling information storage and forgetting) and a learned policy (what to remember, how deeply, and for how long). Our substrate is a fixed-capacity LoRA adapter that consists of k-hot rank combinations which store facts without catastrophic interference and maintain an independently controlled temporal decay, enabling outdated information to fade while long-term facts persist. On this substrate, we train a lightweight controller that governs each incoming user event to ensure the memory system reflects the user's true, updated belief state. To evaluate how memory systems manage user memory, we introduce a controlled dataset of long-horizon user trajectories with varying persistence of facts based on changing belief states and user-behavioral shifts, and probe queries that test recall while information is valid and abstention after it expires. Our policy learns to effectively manage user memory over its entire lifecycle, balancing recall and abstention to achieve 86% of oracle performance, unlike CL and RAG baselines that primarily default to one of the two behaviors. By providing the first system to manage memory directly in model weights, this work establishes a foundation for long-horizon parametric memory.

</details>


### [21] [Moving Generality from Model to Modeler: LLM Agents for PHM on a Deployment-Style Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25252)
*Zhenling Chen,Zhiguo Zeng,Anne Barros*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出部署式基准 NextUnit-Bench（24个公开数据集、58个划分折，留出单元或工况，2小时内交付可执行预测器并在隐藏单元上评测），论证PHM中的"通用性"正从模型迁移到"建模者"(agent)，其优势主要由基于数据做出的设计决策承载，且这种设计只能被能忠实执行它的实现者兑现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有范式是LLM编码代理帮助开发并发布作为通用解的模型；作者追问通用性是否应改置于"为每个新任务构建模型的代理"之上。PHM场景要求每个模型必须在未见设备上工作，是检验该问题的理想试验台。

Method: 构建部署式基准 NextUnit-Bench：24个公开数据集、58折，按单元或工况留出；每个代理限时两小时产出可执行预测器，在隐藏单元上运行。对比对象包括前沿代理、AutoML、以及四个数据集上对已发表方法的忠实复现；并做消融式分析（计划文本迁移、领域工具包、本地可部署开放权重模型执行）。

Result: (1) 前沿代理显著优于AutoML（AutoML不到最佳代理得分的一半），也优于已发表方法的忠实复现（30个中仅6个超过最强代理自设方案）；可复现的论文精度依赖其自身约定，例如对测试机剩余寿命设上限这一单一约定即可使报告误差减小一半。(2) 代理优势来自针对每台机器数据的"设计"：几百字的纯文本计划承载两名前沿代理间大部分差距，较弱代理的计划交给最强代理会使其降至该代理水平甚至预测完全相同；但在接触数据之前，执行谁的计划影响甚微，领域工具包也无明显帮助——最强者的差异在于从数据中学到多少。(3) 设计只能被能执行它的实现者兑现：给定最强代理的设计，本地可部署的开放权重模型交付率仅一半，即使简化设计，得分也不高于无设计时。

Conclusion: 通用性并未消失，而是在转移：从模型转向建模者（modeler/agent）。真正区分强弱的是基于数据的设计能力及其可执行性，而非领域工具或对他人计划的忠实复现。

Abstract: Coding agents built on large language models (LLMs) now help develop many models published as general solutions. Should generality instead reside in the agent that builds a model for each new task? We examine this question in prognostics and health management (PHM), where every model must work on unseen equipment. Our deployment-style benchmark, NextUnit-Bench, covers 24 public datasets split into 58 folds that hold out units or operating conditions; each agent has two hours to deliver an executable predictor, then run on the hidden units. First, frontier agents outperform AutoML, which reaches less than half the best agent's score, and faithful implementations of published methods on four datasets, of which only 6 of 30 beat the strongest agent's own design; published accuracies, where reproducible, rest on the papers' own conventions, and a single convention, capping the remaining life of test engines, can halve the reported error. Second, the agents' advantage is a design made on each machine's data; once made, a plain-text plan of a few hundred words carries most of the gap between two frontier agents, and given a weaker agent's plan, the strongest agent falls to that agent's level, often to identical predictions. Before touching the data, however, it matters little whose plan an agent carries out, and domain toolkits appear to help neither: what sets the strongest apart is how much it learns from the data. Third, such designs reach only implementers that can execute them: given the strongest agent's designs, a locally deployable open-weight model delivers half as often, even when they are lightened, and scores no higher than without them. Generality, we argue, is not disappearing but moving, from the model to the modeler.

</details>


### [22] [Process-Knowledge-Guided Latent Diffusion for Generative Design of Porous Architectures: A Case Study in Aerosol Jet Printing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25264)
*Ngoc Dang Khoa Nguyen,Hyunwoong Ko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出工艺知识引导的潜扩散生成方法，在给定增材工艺设置下生成适配的多孔布局，并以基于流动聚焦关系的依赖工艺利用率评分进行条件生成与梯度引导，实验表明高引导权重显著降低评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 打印多孔布局由功能材料与开放通道交替构成，最佳布局取决于工艺设置如何塑造沉积形貌；现有生成模型生成布局快，但未保证在同一分布下接收各工艺设置的采样，即便依赖工艺的评分会对候选布局给出不同排序。

Method: 定义依赖工艺的利用率评分：由工艺模型设定打印材料效用随距通道距离衰减的长度尺度。生成器融合VQ-VAE潜表征、拓扑U-Net、ControlNet域分支与单独训练的评分回归器；域分支对每步去噪进行条件化，回归器梯度对去噪过程进行引导。以气溶胶喷射打印为例，雾化气与鞘气流量通过流动聚焦关系进入评分。在七个建模拟合流量设置下评估。

Result: 500个设计域中有191个域的最低分候选布局在不同设置间不同（约38%），说明评分排序具设置依赖性。采样研究中，三个验证域与一个测试域上直接计算的均值评分为：仅域条件化0.642，权重300时0.636，权重12000时0.450（每权重112次评分，越低越好）。

Conclusion: 结果支持"工艺知识引导生成"作为在既定假设下进行工艺特定多孔设计的可行途径；但最优权重区间较窄、域条件化本身收益有限，评分仅依赖简化流动聚焦关系，尚需实验验证与更充分的不确定性分析。

Abstract: Printed porous layouts interleave functional material with open channels, and the layout that uses the material best depends on how the process settings shape the deposit. Generative models propose layouts quickly, but a generator that does not receive the process setting samples from the same distribution at every setting, even when the process-dependent score ranks candidate layouts differently. This paper examines whether process knowledge can steer a latent diffusion generator toward layouts suited to a given process setting in a prescribed design domain. We formulate a process-dependent utilization score in which a process model sets the length over which the utility of printed material decays with distance from a channel. The generator combines a vector-quantized variational autoencoder (VQ-VAE) latent representation, a topology U-Net, a ControlNet domain branch, and a separately trained regressor that predicts the score from noised latents; the domain branch conditions each denoising step and the regressor gradient steers it. The method is applied to aerosol jet printing, where the atomizer and sheath gas flows enter the score through the flow-focusing relation. Across seven modeled flow settings, the lowest-scoring candidate layout of a domain differed between settings in 191 of 500 domains. In a sampling study, the mean directly computed score on three validation domains and one test domain was 0.642 with domain conditioning alone, 0.636 at a weight of 300, and 0.450 at 12,000 (112 scores per weight; lower is better). The results support process-knowledge-guided generation as an approach to process-specific porous design under the stated assumptions.

</details>


### [23] [Learning to Verify: Signature-Guided Training for Adaptive Adversarial Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25271)
*Arash Vashagh,Roozbeh Razavi-Far*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"双模型"对抗样本检测方法：固定原分类器，单独训练验证器，利用"激活签名"生成挑战性对抗样本，结合知识蒸馏保持干净精度；在L∞自适应攻击下显著优于被直接攻击的置信度类与特征类检测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗样本检测器在攻击者主动尝试绕过（自适应攻击）时往往失效，缺乏针对定向规避的鲁棒检测机制。

Method: 保持原分类器固定，训练独立验证器评估其预测。训练中识别区分各类真实样本与该类对抗样本的内部激活模式（激活签名），据此生成既误导分类器、又挑战验证器、且在内部表征上接近目标类的困难对抗样本；验证器学习识别这些样本，并通过知识蒸馏在干净输入上保持性能。推理阶段验证器无需激活签名即可评估分类器预测。在公开数据集上针对多种自适应攻击（含训练中未见的攻击）进行评估。

Result: 在所评估的L∞攻击下，该验证器显著优于被直接攻击的基于置信度和基于特征的检测器。消融实验表明，自适应验证器训练是性能提升的主要来源，签名引导主要改善干净样本精度。额外的L2评估揭示了跨扰动类型鲁棒性的局限。

Conclusion: 双模型验证器框架在L∞自适应攻击下有效提升检测鲁棒性，核心贡献来自自适应训练而非签名引导；但鲁棒性对扰动范数类型敏感，L2场景下存在明显局限，泛化性仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: Adversarial example detectors often fail when attackers explicitly attempt to bypass them. We propose a dual-model approach that keeps the original classifier fixed and trains a separate verifier to assess its predictions. During training, we identify patterns of internal neural network activations that distinguish genuine examples of each class from adversarial examples targeting that class. These patterns, called activation signatures, guide the generation of challenging adversarial examples that mislead the classifier, challenge the verifier, and internally resemble the target class. The verifier learns to recognize these examples while preserving its performance on clean inputs through knowledge distillation. At inference, the verifier evaluates the classifier's prediction without requiring activation signatures. We evaluate the approach on publicly available datasets against several adaptive attacks, including attacks unseen during training. The proposed verifier substantially outperforms directly attacked confidence-based and feature-based detectors under the evaluated L-infinity attacks. Ablation studies show that adaptive verifier training is the main source of this improvement, while signature guidance primarily benefits clean accuracy. Additional L2 evaluations reveal limitations in robustness across perturbation types.

</details>


### [24] [Scone: Symmetry-aware Neural Combinatorial Optimization for Graph Coloring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25273)
*Paolo Pellizzoni,Melanie Weber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Scone：利用颜色等变图神经网络序列化构造着色，通过利用颜色置换对称性缩小有效状态空间，同时在需要时打破节点对称性，在多类图着色任务上达到完美解质量且推理时间低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图着色在固定调色板下求无单色边着色是NP难问题，动机在于用学习式方法利用实例间反复出现的结构高效生成高质量着色。关键观察：着色有效性对颜色置换不变，利用该对称性可减小模型需学习策略的有效状态空间；但算法又必须能打破某些对称性（如给同一自同构轨道内相邻节点分配不同颜色）。

Method: Scone模型采用颜色等变图神经网络（color-equivariant GNN），序列化地构造着色，使先前分配的信息能够传播并据此打破节点对称性。

Result: 在多个图着色任务上（包括sudoku_extreme与寄存器分配）实现完美解质量且推理时间低，并展现出对训练中未见图家族的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 将颜色置换对称性纳入模型设计（颜色等变）与序列化构造相结合，是求解图着色的有效学习式范式，兼顾解质量、推理效率与泛化性。

Abstract: Graph coloring, which entails assigning colors to nodes such that no adjacent nodes have the same color, is a fundamental combinatorial optimization problem. In general, however, finding a node coloring with no monochromatic edges given a fixed palette is NP-hard. This motivates learning-based methods that can learn to exploit recurring structure across instances to efficiently produce high-quality colorings. Crucially, the validity of a coloring is invariant to permutations of the colors, and we show that exploiting this symmetry reduces the size of the effective state space over which the model has to learn its policy. At the same time, the coloring algorithm must be able to break some symmetries, for example to assign different colors to two adjacent nodes belonging to the same automorphism orbit. In this paper, we introduce Scone, a model that uses a color-equivariant graph neural network to construct colorings sequentially, allowing information from previous assignments to propagate and break node symmetries. Scone achieves perfect solution quality with low inference times across several graph coloring tasks, including on sudoku_extreme and register allocations, and showcases generalization to graph families not encountered during training.

</details>


### [25] [DS3M: Deep structured state space models for nonlinear system identification on modern hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25274)
*Marco Roschkowski,Hannes Gernandt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出深度结构化状态空间模型 DS3M，以并联与串联的紧凑非线性动力学组件构建，并用 Triton 编写自定义 GPU 内核降低顺序求值的启动与内存开销，从而加速神经状态空间辨识训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SUBNET 将神经状态空间辨识与从短测量历史估计初始状态的编码器结合，但其连续时间版本在数值积分中需大量逐次神经网络求值，训练速度慢，成为实用化的主要瓶颈。

Method: 构建由紧凑非线性动力学组件并联、串联组合的 DS3M；给出其构造方式及 port-Hamiltonian（端口哈密顿）变体；针对顺序求值使用 Triton 实现自定义 GPU 内核以减少内核启动与内存开销。

Result: 在多个基准上对比了精度与训练时间，DS3M 在保持精度的同时显著缩短训练时间（具体数值需见原文）。

Conclusion: 将结构化、可并行的紧凑动力学组件与定制 GPU 内核相结合，可有效缓解连续时间神经状态空间模型训练慢的问题，提升其实用性。

Abstract: Neural state-space identification learns dynamical models from measured inputs and outputs. SUBNET combines this task with an encoder that estimates initial states from short measurement histories. Its continuous-time version requires many successive neural-network evaluations during numerical integration, resulting in slow training speed which is a major limitation for practical use cases. We propose deep structured state space models (DS3M), which combine compact nonlinear dynamical components in parallel and in series. Custom GPU kernels implemented with Triton reduce the launch and memory overhead of their sequential evaluation. We explain this construction, describe a port-Hamiltonian variant, and present various benchmark comparisons of accuracy and training time.

</details>


### [26] [Intelligent Embedding Pruning for Large-Scale Recommendation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25288)
*Anish Khazane,Alex Saad-Falcon,Guanchu Wu,Marcio Porto,Jasper Shan,Fei Yu,Eugene Shulga,Intaik Park,Yongzhe Zhang,Xiaoyu Deng,Sheng Luo,Xiaoya Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 用特征重要性（FI）指导 ITEP 的分表剪枝比例（FI-ITEP），在三个大规模推荐模型上实现 20–40% 总内存降低且精度持平或提升，并转化为更少 GPU trainer 与每模型上线节省 1000+ GPU 小时的系统收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统内存压力主要来自稀疏 embedding 层。现有 in-training embedding pruning（ITEP）虽能在训练中移除不重要的 embedding 行并带来端到端内存节省，但其分表剪枝比例由基于表大小的启发式设定，常导致精度下降。作者希望用更能反映特征价值的信号来设定剪枝比例。

Method: 提出 FI-ITEP：以特征重要性（feature importance）指导各 embedding 表的剪枝比例，对对应特征重要性较低的表施加更高剪枝强度；FI 信号计算开销小。并在开源 DLRM（Criteo 1TB）上复现趋势，量化包含 FI 计算开销在内的端到端训练时间与 GPU 成本权衡。

Result: 在三个大规模推荐模型上，FI-ITEP 相比基于分位数（percentile-based）的 ITEP 实现 20–40% 总内存降低，精度持平或提升；系统层面可用更少 GPU trainer（如 112→80）且单卡吞吐更高，每次模型上线节省 1000+ GPU 小时；DLRM/Criteo 1TB 上复现了相同趋势，并给出端到端时间与成本权衡。

Conclusion: 特征重要性是设定 embedding 剪枝比例的实用信号，能在不损精度的前提下带来显著内存与系统成本收益；论文为真实系统中的 embedding 剪枝提供了可操作的实践指导，并强调 FI 计算的开销可忽略。

Abstract: Modern recommendation systems face substantial memory pressure, largely from the sparse embedding layer that maps high-cardinality categorical features into dense vectors. In-training embedding pruning (ITEP) removes less important embedding rows during training, yielding end-to-end memory savings but sometimes degrading accuracy because per-table pruning ratios are set by size-based heuristics. We use feature importance (FI) to guide these ratios (FI-ITEP), assigning higher pruning to tables tied to less important features. Across three large-scale recommendation models, FI-ITEP achieves 20--40% total memory reduction while matching or improving accuracy relative to percentile-based ITEP. Because the feature-importance signal requires minimal resources to compute, these memory savings translate into concrete systems benefits: the same models train on fewer GPU trainers (e.g., 112 to 80) with higher per-GPU throughput, saving 1,000+ GPU-hours per model launch. We reproduce these trends on the open-source DLRM (Criteo 1TB) and quantify the end-to-end training-time and GPU-cost tradeoff---including the burden of computing FI---offering practical guidance for embedding pruning in real-world systems.

</details>


### [27] [Cross-Question Reliable Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25292)
*Hector G. Rodriguez,Marcus Rohrbach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 CARE（Cross-question Abstention Reward Estimation）奖励方法，用集合内跨题目的弃答阈值来强化 LLM 的置信度估计，使选择性预测在非零风险容忍度下大幅提升覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对 LLM 置信度后训练采用逐题（pointwise）的"正确→置信1、错误→置信0"奖励。理论上最优，但实际中不可达成，且置信度被压到 0/1 两端后丧失题目间的相对可比信息，对非零风险容忍度的选择性预测有害。

Method: CARE 在多组问答对构成的集合上计算奖励：在若干风险容忍度下，由集合内所有置信度导出弃答阈值，对每个置信度按其是否落在阈值正确一侧给予奖励。由于阈值来自集合分布，每个口头化置信度都是相对其他问题的置信度被强化，而不再对齐自身二值正确标签。

Result: 数学任务上，相较逐点正确性匹配奖励，CARE 将 MATH-500、GSM8K、Big-Math-Digits 在 5% 风险下的覆盖率（C@5）从 0.8% 提升至 42.4%。以 HotpotQA 多跳维基问答训练后，在 CommonsenseQA、GPQA、SimpleQA、TriviaQA 等分布外通用知识、推理和数学基准上，C@5 从 12.3% 提升到 23.6%。

Conclusion: 置信度应被训练为可跨题比较的相对信号而非二值标签的拟合，集合级、阈值化的奖励设计能显著改善 LLM 弃答与选择性预测能力，并具备良好的分布外泛化性。

Abstract: AI systems operating near the limits of their capabilities can maximize utility while minimizing the risk of error by abstaining or deferring questions they are not confident about to a human or a stronger model. Recently, large language models (LLMs) have been post-trained to estimate confidence in their own answers. To achieve this, methods reinforce confidence scores based on the correctness signal to each question-answer pair individually, with rewards that encourage assigning confidence 1 to correct answers and 0 to incorrect answers. However, estimating confidence scores is useful in that it enables selecting only the most confident answers, to maximize the share of solved questions while accommodating the user's risk tolerance. Assigning confidence 1 to correct answers and 0 to incorrect ones is theoretically optimal, but unattainable in practice and detrimental for selective prediction with a non-zero risk tolerance: confidences pushed to 0 and 1 carry no information about how answers compare to one another. We therefore introduce CARE (Cross-question Abstention Reward Estimation), which computes rewards over a set of question-answer pairs: At each of several risk tolerances, the confidences across the set induce an abstention threshold, and each confidence is rewarded for lying on the correct side of it. Since the threshold itself comes from the set, every verbalized confidence is reinforced relative to other questions' confidence, not against its own binary correctness label. On math, CARE increases the coverage at 5% risk (C@5) across MATH-500, GSM8K, and Big-Math-Digits from 0.8% to 42.4%, compared to existing pointwise correctness-matching rewards. When trained on multi-hop Wikipedia QA from HotpotQA, CARE improves C@5 in out-of-distribution general-knowledge, reasoning, and math benchmarks (including CommonsenseQA, GPQA, SimpleQA, and TriviaQA) from 12.3% to 23.6%.

</details>


### [28] [Federated Learning of Car Aerodynamics Surrogates with Spectral Optimizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25300)
*Arman Bolatov,Nikolay Spitsyn,Daniil Medyakov,Vladimir Aletov,Dmitry Bylinkin,Martin Takáč,Aleksandr Beznosikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在DrivAerNet++上跨六客户端联邦训练Transolver气动代理模型，发现谱（正交化）优化器FedMuon与FedMARS-M仅需2–4个本地epoch即可达到集中式Muon训练2%以内的阻力误差，而非谱方法需16个epoch仍有17–37%更高误差，原因在于权重梯度稳定秩低、正交化步的一阶下降约为同谱范数梯度步的两倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经代理模型可秒级预测汽车气动力，但训练需数千次各厂商保密的CFD仿真；联邦学习可在不交换数据的前提下联合训练共享代理模型，然而何种联邦优化器能使该代理最接近池化数据训练的集中式模型尚不清楚。

Method: 在DrivAerNet++上，将Transolver代理分布于六个各持单一车身类型的客户端，统一调参协议下比较三种谱优化器（FedMuon及提出的FedMARS-M、FedOLion，对每个权重矩阵的更新做正交化）与三种非谱优化器（FedAvg、SCAFFOLD-Adam、FedLion）。

Result: FedMuon与FedMARS-M在每轮2–4个本地epoch下阻力误差与集中式Muon训练相差2%以内；非谱方法在测试的最大值16个本地epoch下表现最佳，但阻力误差仍比FedMARS-M高17–37%。该差距与权重梯度稳定秩低有关：正交化步的一阶下降约为同谱范数梯度步的两倍。

Conclusion: 谱/正交化联邦优化器在低稳定秩梯度场景下显著优于非谱方法，能以更少本地计算逼近集中式训练性能，为跨厂商协作训练气动代理模型提供了高效方案。

Abstract: Neural surrogates predict the aerodynamic forces on a car in seconds, compared with hours for a CFD simulation, but training them requires thousands of CFD simulations of different car designs, which car manufacturers keep proprietary. Federated learning allows several manufacturers to train a shared surrogate without exchanging data. We study which federated optimizer brings such a surrogate closest to one trained on pooled data. On DrivAerNet++, we train a Transolver surrogate across six clients that each hold cars of a single body type, and we compare three spectral optimizers, which orthogonalize the update of each weight matrix (FedMuon and two methods we propose, FedMARS-M and FedOLion), with three non-spectral ones (FedAvg, SCAFFOLD-Adam and FedLion), all tuned with the same protocol. FedMuon and FedMARS-M reach a drag error within 2% of centralized training with Muon, using two to four local epochs per round. The non-spectral methods perform best with sixteen local epochs, the largest number we tested, and still have a 17 to 37% higher drag error than FedMARS-M. We relate this gap to the low stable rank of the weight gradients, for which an orthogonalized step yields about twice the first-order decrease of a gradient step of the same spectral norm. Code: https://github.com/armanbolatov/fedmars_drivaernet

</details>


### [29] [SplitReAlign: Partition-Aware Representation Realignment for Split Federated Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25301)
*Jia Chen,Yecheng Lv,Xinrong Hu,Jun Bai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SplitReAlign：面向类别级拆分联邦遗忘的分区感知表示重对齐框架，在异构表示空间与非对称更新约束下实现遗忘并保留效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SFL将网络分布于客户端与服务器，类特定知识在双方表示间耦合，使拆分联邦遗忘（SFU）中按类遗忘变得困难：需在异构表示空间与非对称更新约束下去除目标知识同时保留剩余效用。

Method: 构建端点专属判别子空间，经共享语义锚协调；选择性重对齐被遗忘表示并惩罚活跃子空间外的变动。针对非对称更新约束：Client-only更新利用穿过冻结服务器的梯度；Server-only更新利用固定的客户端特征统计提供引导；Joint更新同时优化两侧分区。

Result: 在CIFAR-10与CIFAR-100异构客户端分布下，六种设置中平均与重训练差距最小；Server-only在两个数据集上均与重训练最接近，验证受限更新下分区感知引导的有效性。

Conclusion: 分区感知表示重对齐可有效缓解SFL中类知识耦合带来的遗忘难题，并在非对称更新约束下保持与重训练高度一致，其中Server-only引导尤为有效。

Abstract: Split federated learning (SFL) distributes neural networks across clients and a server, coupling class-specific knowledge across their representations. This coupling complicates split federated unlearning (SFU), where class-wise forgetting must remove target knowledge while preserving retained utility under heterogeneous representation spaces and asymmetric update constraints. We propose SplitReAlign, a partition-aware representation realignment framework for class-wise SFU. It constructs endpoint-specific discriminative subspaces coordinated through a shared semantic anchor, selectively realigning forgotten representations while penalizing changes outside the active subspaces. To accommodate asymmetric update constraints, SplitReAlign leverages gradients through frozen servers for Client-only updates and fixed client-feature statistics for Server-only guidance, while Joint updates optimize both partitions. Experiments on CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100 under heterogeneous client distributions demonstrate that SplitReAlign achieves the lowest average gap to retraining among all compared methods across six settings. Notably, Server-only achieves the closest agreement with retraining on both datasets, highlighting the effectiveness of partition-aware guidance under restricted updates.

</details>


### [30] [Prescribed Bayesian Efficiency under Lifetime Adjustment Costs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25311)
*Viet Bac Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文刻画了贝叶斯学习者在"更新后验的同时保留已发出动作"这一权衡下的最优区域：用带前缀约束的跟踪误差分配准则（及其降权对偶）判定累积期望超额损失与有限期望生命周期调整费用的联合可达性，并给出对数时间下超额损失与必要修订次数的匹配上下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯学习者可在更新后验期间暂不改变已执行的动作，从而以预测损失换取调整（修订）费用。现有分析缺乏对"累积超额损失—调整费用"联合可达性的完整判据，尤其是允许无界或非凸样本损失、任意有界非负非减费用函数的一般性刻画。

Method: 设定总体曲率、边界控制、期望后验不确定性关于最优动作的 order-1/n 下界，以及平方范数一致可积的可观测无偏梯度见证；以累积期望超额（相对当前预测贝叶斯动作）为损失度量。对满足 c(0)=0 的有界非负非减费用 c 与非减剖面 G（G(J)=o(J)），在 T=2^J 时点给出联合可达性的充要条件：跨动作距离尺度的跟踪误差容许量存在前缀约束可行分配，等价于其降权对偶有限。进一步构造与时间跨度无关的确定性检查点（checkpoint）策略，并证明自适应时序与私有随机化不扩大可达区域。

Result: 联合可达性等价于前缀约束分配（或降权对偶）有限；每个可行对均可由一个与地平线无关的确定性检查点策略达到，且生命周期费用具有任意正阶矩。对于超额 O((log T)^r)（0<r<1），任何策略期望修订次数为 Ω((log T)^{2-r})，且存在与费用无关的调度达到匹配的逐路径上界。假设在非退化协变量、有限二阶矩、局部正光滑先验密度的 logistic 决策模型（含带无界参数先验的固定岭模型）中得到验证。

Conclusion: 论文给出贝叶斯动作保留问题中损失—费用联合可达性的完整充要判据（前缀约束分配/对偶形式），证明检查点型确定性策略即可实现最优剖面，且自适应与随机化无增益；同时确立对数超额损失下修订次数的紧阶 Ω((log T)^{2-r}) 与匹配上界，并将理论框架验证于一类含无界先验的 logistic 决策模型。

Abstract: A Bayesian learner may retain an issued action while updating its posterior, trading predictive loss against adjustment charges. We assume population curvature, boundary control, an order-$1/n$ lower bound on expected posterior uncertainty about the optimal action, and an observable unbiased gradient witness whose squared norms are uniformly integrable. Sample losses may be unbounded or nonconvex. Cumulative expected excess measures the loss increase relative to current predictive Bayes actions. For bounded nonnegative nondecreasing charges $c$ with $c(0)=0$ and nondecreasing profiles $G: \mathbb{N} \to [1,\infty)$ with $G(J)=o(J)$, we characterize joint attainability of cumulative expected excess $O(G(J))$ at horizons $T=2^J$ and finite expected lifetime charge. Here $J$ indexes logarithmic time. The criterion is finiteness of a prefix-constrained allocation of tracking-error allowances across action-distance scales, equivalently its decreasing-weight dual. Adaptive timing and private randomization do not enlarge the attainable region. For each feasible pair, a horizon-independent deterministic checkpoint policy attains the profile with lifetime-cost moments of every positive order. For excess $O((\log T)^r)$, $0<r<1$, every policy needs $Ω((\log T)^{2-r})$ revisions in expectation, and a charge-independent schedule achieves a matching pathwise upper bound for every feasible charge. We verify the assumptions for logistic decision models with nondegenerate covariates, finite second moments and a locally positive smooth prior density, including fixed-ridge models with unbounded parameter priors.

</details>


### [31] [Improving Imitation Policies for Multi-Phase Tasks through Residual Learning Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25320)
*Clinton Enwerem,Saleh Kemal,John S. Baras,Calin Belta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"完成增益"（completion gain）指标，用于在残差强化学习中分配有限的交互预算与决定修正动作分量，并基于配对回合进行估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 残差RL需在有限交互预算下修正模仿学习基策略；多阶段任务中预算须跨阶段分配，而基策略失败最多的阶段未必是修正后最能提升整体完成率的阶段，二者需明确区分。

Method: 定义完成增益为"在单个阶段以状态反馈控制器替换基策略选定动作分量时全任务完成概率的变化"，并将其分解为到达该阶段的概率与自该阶段起的条件完成改进，用配对回合（paired episodes）估计；正增益估计决定残差策略修正哪些动作分量以及训练回合在各阶段的分配。

Result: 随机点质量对接实验中，多数失败出现在最终对接阶段，但最大完成增益出现在前置通道阶段；基于增益的分配优于按失败计数比例的分配，且在更长接近的变体中以一半预算即优于均匀分配。仿真双臂装配中，增益估计仅在插入与释放阶段为正（尽管释放阶段失败很少）；在第二种装配配置下，于释放阶段训练的残差策略使留出状态起点的完成率提升16.9%。

Conclusion: 失败分布与完成增益可显著错位，以完成增益为准则进行预算与动作分量分配能更高效地利用有限交互预算提升全任务完成率。

Abstract: Residual reinforcement learning corrects an imitation-trained base policy using a limited budget of environment interaction. In a task with several phases, this budget must be divided among the phases, and the phase in which the base policy fails is not necessarily the phase in which a correction most improves task completion. We formalize this distinction through the completion gain: the change in full-task completion probability when, during a single phase, a state-feedback controller replaces selected components of the base policy's action. We decompose the completion gain into the probability of reaching the phase and the conditional improvement in completion from the start of the phase, and we estimate it from paired episodes. The positive estimates determine which action components the residual policy corrects and how training episodes are distributed across phases. In stochastic point-mass docking experiments, most failures occurred in the final docking phase, whereas the largest completion gain occurred in the preceding channel phase; gain-based allocation improved completion over allocation proportional to failure counts and, in a variant with a longer approach, over uniform allocation at half of the budget. In simulated bimanual robotic assembly, the completion-gain estimates were positive only for insertion and release, although few failures occurred during release; in a second assembly configuration, a residual policy trained in the release phase improved completion from held-out states at the start of release by 16.9%.

</details>


### [32] [Now You See Me Now You Don't: A Framework To Detect and Reduce Proxy Capacity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25327)
*Nirek Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出“代理容量量化检验 + 代理特征预处理调整”的两段式框架，在不删除特征的前提下降低差别对待风险并保留任务相关信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受监管领域（如信贷）中差别影响已有成熟的量化检测与缓解方法，但差别对待的量化研究不足。实践中代理变量识别依赖条件独立性检验的构建方式，缺乏可规模化、可辩护的框架；且现有做法多为直接删除被判定为代理的特征，导致预测性能下降（fairness through unawareness）。

Method: 第一部分：提出代理容量（proxy capacity）的定量检验，判断特征的某些取值是否泄露特定人口群体的成员身份；第二部分：提出预处理方法，对这些代理特征进行调整以降低其代理容量，而非直接删除。

Result: 在合成数据集与公开数据集上的实证表明，该框架能识别出对人口信息具有代理作用的特征并降低其代理容量，同时调整后的特征保留任务相关信号，预测性能优于直接删除特征。

Conclusion: 为差别对待提供可量化的代理识别与缓解工具，兼顾公平性与预测效用，为ML系统在受监管场景中的规模化合规审查提供可辩护路径。

Abstract: In regulated domains such as lending, the two most salient fairness concepts are disparate impact and disparate treatment: disparate impact is generally defined by differential rates of adverse outcomes between demographic groups, while disparate treatment is generally defined by the use of demographic information or proxies to issue decisions. There already exist established methods to quantitatively detect and mitigate disparate impact; however, there has been less research on doing the same for disparate treatment. From a practitioner's perspective, the manner in which a conditional independence test is formulated and operationalized to identify proxies is nuanced and underexplored. As Machine Learning (ML) systems increase in scope and application in regulated domains, practitioners need a defensible framework to identify proxies at scale. Current disparate treatment frameworks typically rely upon some combination of quantitative and qualitative review of models' inputs to identify proxies. Features that are identified as proxies are then typically removed from the model in practice, and predictive performance degrades in favor of fairness through unawareness. Here, we propose a two-part framework that reduces the disparate treatment risk of a feature and keeps the predictive performance that removal of the feature loses. First, we propose a quantitative test for proxy capacity that identifies whether some of a feature's values reveal membership in a particular demographic group. Second, we propose a pre-processing methodology that adjusts those proxy features to reduce their proxy capacity. We show empirically on synthetic and public datasets that this framework identifies features that proxy for demographic information and reduces their proxy capacity while the adjusted feature retains task-relevant signal (i.e. improves predictive performance versus removal).

</details>


### [33] [Class-Conditional Conformal Triage for ICAO Face-Image Compliance under Class Imbalance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25342)
*Mustafa Vedat Yildirim,Inna Skarga-Bandurova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文将保形预测（conformal prediction）作为自动化ICAO/ISO人脸图像合规分析器的三级分诊层，在TONO基准上对14项合规准则做边际与Mondrian（标签条件）保形预测与弃权规则的对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合规分析器只给出准则级通过/拒绝判定，在"接受/拒绝/重拍"决策中缺乏不确定性量化。

Method: 在BioGaze上免重训练：为每项准则拟合逻辑回归分类器，采用主体间不相交划分，比较边际保形预测、Mondrian（标签条件）保形预测与传统弃权规则，并对14项准则做Bonferroni联合覆盖校正。

Result: 边际保形总体覆盖率约0.90，但违规类别覆盖严重不足（某准则十折均值为0.37）；Mondrian各准则均值0.89–0.92。假阳性下降主要来自逻辑回归层（0.145→0.048），且在相近重拍率下调优弃权规则的假阳性率低于Mondrian；Bonferroni联合覆盖要求几乎重拍所有图像。

Conclusion: 类条件保形预测可为少数违规类接近目标经验覆盖率，并揭示统计覆盖、分类性能与重拍需求之间的多准则权衡。

Abstract: Automated ICAO/ISO face-image compliance analysers provide criterion-level pass/fail decisions, yet these verdicts do not quantify uncertainty when deciding whether to accept, reject, or retake a photograph. We investigate conformal prediction as a triage layer for BioGaze, evaluating 14 compliance criteria on the synthetic TONO benchmark. Without retraining the analyser, we fit per-criterion logistic classifiers and compare marginal and label-conditional (Mondrian) conformal prediction with conventional abstention rules using subject-disjoint splits. Marginal conformal prediction achieved approximately 0.90 overall coverage but substantially under-covered violations, with a ten-partition mean of 0.37 for one criterion. The corresponding Mondrian means ranged from 0.89 to 0.92 across criteria. However, most of the reduction in criterion-level false positives came from the logistic layer (0.145 to 0.048), while tuned abstention rules achieved lower false-positive rates than Mondrian at comparable retake rates. Bonferroni correction for joint coverage across 14 criteria required retaking nearly all images. These findings show that class-conditional conformal prediction can achieve near-target empirical coverage for minority violation classes and reveal trade-offs between statistical coverage, classification performance, and retake requirements in multi-criterion face-image compliance assessment.

</details>


### [34] [Drifting, Discriminating, and Directioning for Action Coordination in Higher-dimensional Action Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25385)
*Taisuke Kobayashi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量级 drifting 策略模型及三步训练算法，用于高维动作空间强化学习中的动作协调建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在高维动作空间中难以有效处理动作分布建模，而单纯用 Q 梯度反向传播训练 drifting 模型无法充分捕捉所需的动作协调（action coordination）。

Method: 三步学习算法：(1) 将 drifting 模型的源分布作为对角策略模型共同优化，使 drifting 模型以残差形式承载对角分布无法表达的动作协调；(2) 引入 drifting 前/后的判别器与对抗训练作为正则项，主动促进对区分两分布的关键协调特征的提取；(3) 在 rollout 动作选择中采用 best-of-N 采样，为协调未充分提取/已充分发现的区域提供方向性引导。策略模型本身为轻量级、单次前向传播推断。

Result: 在三个高维动作空间的运动（locomotion）任务上，相较传统对角分布建模，方法一致提升样本效率与最终控制性能；分析表明改进源于降低某些动作维度的方差，并将其与其他动作有效耦合为动作协调。

Conclusion: drifting 策略模型配合三步训练可有效实现高维动作空间下的动作协调建模，兼顾轻量化与性能提升。

Abstract: This paper proposes a lightweight policy model to mitigate the difficulty of handling higher-dimensional action spaces in reinforcement learning (RL), alongside an algorithm to effectively train it. To address this, we focus on drifting models, which can implicitly represent complex distributions and infer actions in a single forward pass. However, while drifting models can be straightforwardly trained via backpropagation of the Q-gradient, this alone cannot be expected to appropriately capture the desired action coordination. Therefore, this paper constructs a three-step learning algorithm to encourage the action coordination. First, the source distribution of the drifting model is co-optimized as a diagonal policy model. This conditions the drifting model to attach the coordination, which cannot be expressed by a diagonal distribution, as residuals. Second, a discriminator model before and after drifting is introduced with adversarial training for a regularization term. This actively promotes the extraction of the coordination that fundamentally separates the two distributions. Third, best-of-N sampling is applied for action selection during rollouts in order to give directionality toward/beyond the regions where the coordination is insufficiently extracted/sufficiently discovered. The proposed method consistently improves the sample efficiency and the final control performance over the conventional diagonal distribution modeling across three locomotion tasks with high-dimensional action spaces. Furthermore, the analysis indicates that this improvement is achieved by reducing variance in certain action dimensions, effectively coupling them with other actions as action coordination.

</details>


### [35] [TuringSpace: Grounding Spatial Intelligence in Action with Interactive CAPTCHAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25387)
