Ablation experiments show that removing mass-distribution descriptors reduces magnitude R^2 to 0.900, while removing build-plate contact and overhang descriptors reduces it to 0.920. These results demonstrate the value of combining local mesh connectivity with global geometric descriptors to predict LPBF distortion, enabling faster design iteration and earlier identification of distortion-prone geometries.

</details>


### [103] [OceanCoder: A Verification-Guided Large Language Model for Automated Simulation Modelling in Ocean Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26333)
*Can Xue,Changhong He,Ziyi Sun,Anliang Ge,Shimin Yu,Bingchen Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OceanCoder，基于Qwen3-8B构建海洋工程自动仿真建模大模型，通过可扩展数据构建管线生成12000条SFT样本，并以三层工程验证框架提供分级奖励驱动GRPO强化学习，同时构建首个海洋工程自动建模基准，Pass@1由基座0%提升至SFT后46.77%、GRPO后51.43%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海洋工程数值仿真建模依赖专家知识；现有LLM自动建模仅关注代码语法正确与可执行，无法保证生成的模型与结果满足工程需求，且领域专用编程需求强、训练数据稀缺。

Method: 基于Qwen3-8B；构建可扩展数据管线，覆盖12类工程任务、产出12000条经校验的SFT样本；设计三层验证框架（输入是否符合工程参数与任务约束、所需仿真分析是否完成、输出结果的工程合理性），其结果为GRPO提供分级奖励；并构建首个涵盖分布外任务与真实工程案例的自动建模基准。

Result: 实验与消融验证了数据管线、验证框架与奖励设计的有效性；OceanCoder优于基座模型与闭源对比模型，Pass@1从基座0%升至SFT后46.77%、GRPO后51.43%。

Conclusion: 工程验证可作为自动仿真建模的有效训练信号，将评价标准从"代码可运行"推进到"工程任务完成"。

Abstract: Numerical simulation is important in ocean engineering, but model construction requires expert knowledge. Large language models support automated modelling, but code syntax and successful execution alone do not establish whether generated models and results meet engineering requirements. We present OceanCoder, a Qwen3-8B-based large language model for automated simulation modelling in ocean engineering. To address specialised programming requirements and scarce training data, we develop an extensible data construction pipeline producing 12,000 verified supervised fine-tuning (SFT) examples across 12 engineering task categories. A three-layer verification framework checks model inputs against engineering parameters and task constraints, verifies completion of required simulation analyses, and assesses the engineering plausibility of computed outputs. Its outcomes provide graded rewards for group relative policy optimisation (GRPO), guiding the model towards engineering task completion. We also construct the first benchmark for automated modelling in ocean engineering, covering out-of-distribution tasks and real-world engineering cases. Experiments and ablation studies demonstrate the effectiveness of the data pipeline, verification framework and reward design. OceanCoder outperforms both base models and the closed-source comparison, with Pass@1 increasing from 0% for the base model to 46.77% after SFT and 51.43% after GRPO. These results support engineering verification as a training signal for automated simulation modelling.

</details>


### [104] [TRCF: Temporal-Reference Complementary Forecasting for Irregular Multivariate Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26351)
*Xudong Zuo,Shusen Ma,Qirui Chen,Bing Fan,Yun-Bo Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TRCF 通过为紧凑的通道独立预测器附加互补的通道依赖分支，在不规则多变量时间序列预测中兼顾跨变量信息与各变量不规则历史，取得最低 MSE/MAE 并使峰值推理内存较 APN 降低 17.28%–87.98%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不规则多变量时间序列预测中，通道独立（CI）方法能刻画每个变量的时序模式但不显式利用其他变量观测；通道依赖（CD）方法虽可建模变量间关系，却因异步采样难以学习。如何在保留各变量不规则历史的同时引入跨变量信息是核心挑战。

Method: 提出 TRCF 框架，由紧凑 CI 预测器加互补 CD 分支构成。ANCHOR：在共享时间位置聚合观测值与时间戳，保留观测支撑以形成紧凑 CI 特征。BRIDGE：通过历史补全构建互补特征，先由变量自身观测估计其历史值，再利用其他变量在邻近时刻的观测值与估计值之差修正缺失项；补全后的历史保留原始观测值并被编码，作为上下文与直接 CI 表示并列。最后融合两种表示并结合查询时刻嵌入，输入浅层 MLP 进行预测。

Result: 在四个真实数据集上，TRCF 在主基准对比中获得最低的 MSE 与 MAE；相对 APN，实测峰值推理内存降低 17.28% 至 87.98%。

Conclusion: TRCF 表明，以 CI 为主干、CD 为互补分支的设计可有效缓解异步测量下的跨变量建模难题，在提升预测精度的同时显著降低推理内存开销。

Abstract: Irregular multivariate time series forecasting supports healthcare monitoring, environmental analysis, and activity recognition. Channel-independent (CI) approaches capture each variable's temporal patterns but do not explicitly use observations from other variables. Channel-dependent (CD) modeling exploits relationships among variables, yet asynchronous measurements make these relationships difficult to learn. Incorporating cross-variable information while preserving each variable's irregular history therefore remains a key challenge. We propose Temporal-Reference Complementary Forecasting (TRCF), a framework that augments a compact CI forecaster with a complementary CD branch. ANCHOR aggregates observed values and timestamps at shared temporal locations, retaining observation support to form compact CI features. BRIDGE constructs complementary features through history completion. It first estimates each variable's historical values from its own observations, then uses differences between other variables' observed and estimated values at nearby times to correct missing entries. The completed history preserves observed values and is encoded to supply context alongside the direct CI representation. Finally, the two representations are fused, combined with the query-time embedding, and passed to a shallow multilayer perceptron for forecasting. Experiments on four real-world datasets show that TRCF achieves the lowest mean squared error and mean absolute error in the main benchmark comparison, while reducing measured peak inference memory by 17.28% to 87.98% relative to APN.

</details>


### [105] [Mamba Flow Matching Neural Processes: Linear-Time Inference for Irregularly Observed Spatial Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26355)
*Cosmo Santoni,Giovanni Charles,Timothy James Hitge,Oliver Watson,Elizaveta Semenova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 MambaFlowNP：一种基于多方向 Mamba-2 扫描与条件流匹配（CFM）训练的联合预测神经过程，线性复杂度、一次前向即可对稀疏不规则观测场进行插值，在 NOAA 温度插值上取得最低 RMSE/CRPS 且比最强注意力基线快 114–784 倍，单卡可扩展至 10^6 点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统高斯过程克里金每个新场都需重估超参并求解 O(N^3) 系统；联合预测型神经过程（NP）虽可通过一次前向预测新场，但其对上下文与查询点做自注意力，复杂度为 O(K^2)，实际只能处理数千点；而直接的状态空间改造会把上下文压缩为单一隐状态，削弱解码器可获取的信息。

Method: MambaFlowNP 采用条件流匹配训练联合预测 NP：将上下文与查询 token 放入同一共享序列，用多方向 Mamba-2 扫描线性时间地读取；每个查询的速度场由其自身扫描位置读出而非压缩后的上下文摘要；由于查询只读取扫描状态，预测对查询集划分保持一致。

Result: 在合成 Matérn 高斯过程基准上与最强注意力联合预测基线相当；在区域至大陆尺度 NOAA 温度插值各层级上取得最低 RMSE 与 CRPS 并接近名义覆盖率，速度比最强联合预测基线快 114–784 倍；单张 NVIDIA L40S 上一次前向不到 1 秒即可扩展到 10^6 点，预测质量评估至 K=5,580。

Conclusion: 线性计算的联合预测 NP 使大陆尺度、不规则采样的传感器网络插值变得可行，兼顾预测精度、概率校准与计算效率。

Abstract: Interpolating a spatial field, such as daily maximum temperature, from sparse, irregularly located observations recurs across the environmental and health sciences. Standard Gaussian-process kriging refits hyperparameters and solves an $O(N^3)$ system for every new field. When many related fields are observed, such as daily maps from one sensor network, neural processes (NPs) instead learn the interpolation map from past fields and predict a new field in one forward pass. Joint-predictive variants, which sample whole fields rather than independent per-location values, typically use self-attention over context and query points, costing $O(K^2)$ in the combined point count $K$ and limiting practical use to a few thousand points. State-space models offer a linear-time alternative, but a direct state-space adaptation compresses the context into one hidden state before reading queries, reducing what the decoder sees. We propose MambaFlowNP, a linear-compute joint-predictive NP trained with conditional flow matching. A multi-directional Mamba-2 scan reads context and query tokens in one shared sequence, so each query's velocity is read from its own scan position rather than a compressed context summary, and because queries only read the scan state, predictions are consistent across query sets. MambaFlowNP is competitive with the strongest attention-based joint-predictive baselines on synthetic Matérn Gaussian-process benchmarks, and on regional to continental-scale NOAA temperature interpolation it attains the lowest RMSE and CRPS and near-nominal coverage on every tier, while running 114--784$\times$ faster than the strongest joint-predictive baseline. A single forward pass scales to $10^6$ points on one NVIDIA L40S GPU in under a second. This makes joint prediction feasible for continental-scale, irregularly sampled sensor networks; predictive quality is evaluated up to $K=5{,}580$.

</details>


### [106] [Learning from Context: Leave-One-Out Prediction in Jump Diffusions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26359)
*Christian Wald*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究嵌入空间中带坐标重置的跳跃扩散（jump diffusion）离散生成模型。作者提出用留一法（LOO）预测（每个坐标仅由其他坐标的加噪状态预测）来迫使模型学习坐标间依赖，并证明在完整坐标重置与状态无关时钟下，基于精确LOO概率的跳跃扩散可保持规定边际分布；同时证明端点后验漂移可由LOO概率经解析似然补全构造，无需额外网络评估。在Text8、LM1B文本与ZINC250k分子图生成上，LOO变体一致优于匹配的无限制基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续扩散模型通过嵌入空间中的随机动力学生成离散数据，但坐标自身的加噪状态会"泄漏"答案，使预测过于容易，从而降低学习坐标间依赖关系的压力，导致生成质量受限。

Method: 提出带坐标到类别值嵌入重置的跳跃扩散框架；采用leave-one-out（LOO）预测，即每个坐标仅由其余坐标的损坏状态预测；在完整坐标重置与状态无关时钟假设下证明规定边际保持性；并证明可用LOO概率通过解析似然补全构造完整端点后验漂移，无需额外网络前向计算；在Text8、LM1B及ZINC250k上进行对比实验，并通过受控干预检验模型对局部输入与上下文的依赖。

Result: LOO变体在三个数据集上均优于匹配的无限制基线，改进了关键生成指标；受控干预实验显示模型对局部输入与上下文的依赖发生变化；解析似然补全的效果亦得到分析。

Conclusion: 以LOO预测替代无限制自预测可在不增加网络评估成本的前提下提升离散扩散生成的性能，且理论上仍保持正确的边缘分布，为训练信号设计与效率兼顾提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Continuous diffusion models generate discrete data through stochastic dynamics in an embedding space. We consider jump diffusions that additionally reset coordinates to embeddings of categorical values. A coordinate's own corrupted state can make prediction easy, potentially reducing the pressure to learn dependencies between coordinates. We study leave-one-out (LOO) prediction, in which each coordinate is predicted from the corrupted states of the other coordinates. We show that, under stated assumptions, jump diffusions with complete coordinate resets and state-independent clocks preserve the prescribed marginals when reset destinations use exact LOO probabilities and the drift uses the full endpoint posterior. We further show that this drift can be constructed from LOO probabilities by analytic likelihood completion without additional network evaluations. We evaluate unrestricted, LOO, and completed predictions on Text8 and LM1B text generation and ZINC250k molecular graph generation. LOO-based variants improve key generation metrics over matched unrestricted controls on all three datasets. Controlled interventions assess reliance on local inputs and context, and we analyze the effect of analytic likelihood completion.

</details>


### [107] [HiSA: Sparsifying the Hidden Dimension of RoPE Attention in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26363)
*Ambroise Borbely,Mathieu Salzmann,Olivier Verscheure*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Hidden Sparse Attention (HiSA)，沿隐藏（特征）维度而非 token 维度做静态、免参数的稀疏化：保留 query/key 投影完整，但只计算部分点积，基于 RoPE 结构将 query/key 分解可省略部分；从头训练 LLM 时性能不降反升，仅需约 1/3 的 query-key 乘积，推理更快更省显存，并扩展到 FlashAttention-3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏注意力几乎都在 token 维度（序列维度）做稀疏，隐藏（特征）维度的稀疏性探索很少；作者质疑注意力是否必须计算完整点积才能有效，尝试在特征维度上省略部分点积。

Method: HiSA 保持 query/key 投影不变，仅按 token 位置设定静态、免参数的规则计算部分点积；利用 RoPE 的位置结构把 key 与 query 分解为可省略的部分，从而在隐藏维度上实现稀疏注意力；训练端与推理端均实现，并移植到 FlashAttention-3 kernel；通过从头训练 LLM 验证，并用稠密注意力对 HiSA 丢弃分数的抑制现象给出动机解释。

Result: 从头训练时，部分点积相比完整点积性能不降反升，query-key 乘积计算量仅约 1/3；推理阶段提升解码速度并减小显存占用；FlashAttention-3 实现可支撑训练与推理。

Conclusion: 注意力无需完整点积即可有效，隐藏维度稀疏是一条被忽视但有效的稀疏化路径；稠密注意力本身会学习抑制 HiSA 所丢弃的注意力分数部分，为方法提供了合理性依据。

Abstract: Attention in large language models (LLMs) scores every query against every key with a full dot product. We investigate whether this dot product must be computed completely for attention to be effective. We propose Hidden Sparse Attention (HiSA), which keeps the query and key projections intact but only computes a partial dot product, following a static, parameter-free rule set by the token positions: attention becomes sparse in the hidden (feature) dimension, an axis much less explored than token sparsity, rather than over tokens as in almost all sparse attention methods. Based on the structure of rotary position embeddings (RoPE), we decompose keys and queries into parts which can be omitted when computing attention. By training LLMs from scratch with HiSA, we find that this partial dot product does not hurt but improves performance compared with the complete product while computing only about a third of the query-key products. During inference HiSA improves decoding speed and shrinks memory footprint. We also extend HiSA to FlashAttention-3 kernels for training and inference. Finally, we motivate our approach by showing that dense attention learns to suppress the attention score parts that HiSA drops. Code is available at https://github.com/ambroise-b/hisa.

</details>


### [108] [From Benchmarks to the Bench: Preference, Correspondence and Selectivity in Bidirectional Drug-Target Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26369)
*Jiangjie Qiu,Chengrui Xu,Xiaonan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较大 13 个已发布的药物-靶标相互作用（DTI）预测系统的双向推荐一致性，并实证检验其预测能否指导实验决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DTI 模型可对海量药物-靶标对排序，但实验验证通量有限，仅有少数候选能进入测试。因此，仅报告基准指标不足，需评估不同系统推荐之间的一致性，以及推荐是否真正富集活性候选。

Method: 在 711 个 FDA 获批药物分子结构与 614 个人类蛋白上，比较 13 个已发布系统的双向 Top-10 推荐；对 79 个分子、65 个激酶的已发表抑制测量数据计算命中率（hit yield），并做推荐与不同药物/靶标匹配的置换式评估；另对同一架构、同一数据、固定训练目标仅改变随机种子重新训练，考察结果稳定性。

Result: 同一靶标或药物下，77.8% 与 70.1% 的模型对比给出完全不相交的 Top-10 分子/蛋白列表；部分重叠主要源于对相同候选的重复偏好。抑制实验数据显示，部分系统能富集活性候选，但将推荐匹配到不同药物或靶标时命中率变化很小（提示其富集可能并非靶标特异），另一些系统命中损失显著；前者中亦有系统在两药物偏好不同激酶时很少同时正确排序分子与激酶。相同架构仅换随机种子，测试性能相近，但在独立筛选目录上的平均两两 Top-10 重叠仅约 2 个候选。

Conclusion: DTI 模型评估应从基准性能转向“能否指导有意义的实验决策”，当前系统的推荐一致性与稳定性不足，单看基准分数会掩盖其在药物发现与靶标鉴定中的实用性差异。

Abstract: Identifying active molecules for known targets and potential targets for existing drugs is central to drug discovery and repurposing. Drug-target interaction (DTI) models can rank vast numbers of drug-target pairs, but limited experimental capacity restricts testing to a small number of candidates. We compared the bidirectional recommendations of 13 released systems across 711 molecular structures of FDA-approved drugs and 614 human proteins. For the same target or drug, pairs of models produced completely disjoint Top-10 molecule and protein lists in 77.8% and 70.1% of comparisons, respectively. Even where recommendations overlapped, agreement between some systems largely reflected recurring preferences for the same candidates. Published inhibition measurements for 79 molecules and 65 kinases revealed that some systems enriched active candidates, yet their hit yields changed little when recommendations were matched to different drugs or targets, whereas other systems lost substantially more hits. Some of the former systems also rarely ranked both molecules and kinases correctly when two drugs favoured different kinases. We further retrained the same architecture on the same data, with the training objective fixed and random seeds varied. Despite similar test performance, mean pairwise Top-10 overlap on a separate screening catalogue was about two candidates. Our study moves DTI model evaluation beyond benchmark performance to assess whether model predictions can guide meaningful experimental decisions in drug discovery and target identification.

</details>


### [109] [Evidence from a Multi-Regime Causal Discovery Benchmark: UCMT Algorithms, Comparative Performance, and Controlled Ablation Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26383)
*Mahyar Eslami*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对UCMT因果发现算法家族进行内部、预注册的分层评估，并单独预注册控制实验检验UCMT v2 Global各组件贡献；结论支持UCMT为"已实现并经经验研究的算法家族"，而非整体优越性主张。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果发现算法缺乏对同一算法家族内部不同配置的家族分层、预注册比较，以及"哪个内部阶段真正影响结果"的针对性证据；作者希望给出经验与方法论双重贡献，并避免过度宣称。

Method: 在异构基准体系（真实、半合成、高斯网络、iSCM、CSuite等）上做主基准对比，将三种活跃开发的UCMT配置按家族分层，并与NOTEARS、DAGMA、BOSS、置换搜索方法及R^2-SortnRegress方向基线比较；采用配对运行级分析与数据集级Holm多重检验校正；另设固定骨架消融实验考察sparsity、BIC精化、仲裁与自动机制选择等内部阶段。

Result: 主基准中UCMT v2 Global在部分真实/半合成/高斯网络配对比较中有向F1高于NOTEARS与DAGMA，但落后于领先的置换搜索方法；UCMT v4对BOSS在CSuite上有向F1平均配对差+0.279（配对Holm p<0.001），但数据集级校正后不显著（p=0.188）；固定骨架消融中UCMT v2 Global在标准化iSCM上优于改编R^2-SortnRegress（F1 0.537 vs 0.468，数据集级Holm p=0.031），绝对方向准确率0.563，对RESIT、ANM、DirectLiNGAM无广泛优势；sparsity与BIC精化对部分结果有实质影响，仲裁与自动机制选择未获确认的普遍提升。

Conclusion: 结论审慎：UCMT是被实现并经经验调查的算法家族，结果不支持其整体优越性、独立外部验证或商业影响；论文同时报告性能短板、多重检验校正、运行成本、评估局限与可复现性检查。

Abstract: We present an internal, pre-registered evaluation of the UCMT causal discovery algorithm family alongside established alternatives across heterogeneous benchmark regimes, complemented by a separately pre-registered, controlled study of UCMT v2 Global components. The contribution is both empirical and methodological: a family-stratified comparison of three actively developed algorithm configurations and targeted evidence about which internal stages affect their outcomes. In the main benchmark, UCMT v2 Global has higher directed F1 than NOTEARS and DAGMA in selected real, semi-synthetic and Gaussian-network comparisons at the paired-run level, while trailing leading permutation-search methods in other settings. The BOSS-based UCMT v4 produces a CSuite-specific directed-F1 improvement over BOSS (mean paired difference +0.279; pair-level Holm p < 0.001), but the dataset-level adjusted result is not significant (p = 0.188). In a separate fixed-skeleton ablation, UCMT v2 Global exceeds an adapted R^2-SortnRegress orientation baseline on standardized iSCM data (directed F1 0.537 vs 0.468, dataset-level Holm p = 0.031); absolute orientation accuracy is 0.563 and there is no established broad advantage over RESIT, ANM or DirectLiNGAM. Controlled ablations show that sparsity and BIC-based refinement materially affect some outcomes, whereas the examined arbitration and automatic regime-selection mechanisms do not yield confirmed general improvements. We report performance shortcomings, multiple-testing correction, runtime costs, evaluation limitations and reproducibility checks. The results support UCMT as an implemented and empirically investigated algorithm family, not a claim of overall superiority, independent validation or demonstrated commercial impact.

</details>


### [110] [A Complete Geometric Message-Passing Architecture for Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26384)
*Nadav Dym,Tal Amir,Gia Chi Dang,Christopher Morris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新颖的等变消息传递点云架构，并证明其具有完备性（可区分任何不被刚性运动与置换对称性关联的输入），实验表明其能区分距离型消息传递无法区分的点云，且无需高阶表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点云架构家族多样且复杂度、计算代价差异大，完备性（表达力）是原则性比较标准。已有的距离型消息传递网络不完备，而现有完备替代方案依赖计算密集的高阶旋转群表示或高阶 Weisfeiler-Leman 型测试，代价高昂。

Method: 设计一种简单的新型等变消息传递架构，通过理论证明其完备性；并开展实验验证其对困难点云的分离能力，同时通过证明略微修改版 SEGNN 的完备性给出初步证据。

Result: 理论证明所提架构完备；实验显示其能分离距离型消息传递可证明无法区分的挑战性点云；并证明了修改版 SEGNN 的完备性。

Conclusion: 高阶等变特征可能并非提升点云架构表达力的必要条件；该方法可为其他尚不完备的等变架构的完备性证明提供思路。

Abstract: Three-dimensional point clouds are a fundamental data modality, and a large family of machine learning architectures has been developed to respect their natural symmetries to rigid motions and permutations. A principled way to compare these architectures, which vary widely in complexity and cost, is through their expressive power. That is, an architecture is complete if it can distinguish any two inputs not related by the symmetries it respects. Simple distance-based message-passing networks are known to be incomplete, and while there exist complete alternatives, they typically rely on computationally intensive mechanisms like high-order representations of the rotation group or higher-order Weisfeiler-Leman-type tests.
  In this work, we devise a novel, simple, equivariant message-passing architecture for point clouds, and prove that it is complete. We complement our theoretical result with experiments showing that our architecture separates challenging point clouds that distance-based message passing provably cannot distinguish. Our results suggest that high-order equivariant features may not be necessary to enhance the expressivity of point cloud architectures, and can help settle completeness for other equivariant architectures for which such results are currently unknown. We give initial evidence for this by proving completeness of a slightly modified version of SEGNN.

</details>


### [111] [Sequential Anytime Valid Tests for Efficient Inference-Time Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26413)
*Vishesh Kumar Singh,Sayak Ray Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 e-RWSC，一种 anytime-valid 的自适应停止规则：以误差容忍度 α 为输入，用针对每个答案的 betting supermartingale（e-process）替代 Wald 的 SPRT，在时间与答案维度上一致地控制"误认证非主流答案"的概率不超过 α，从而在显著降低 token 成本的同时提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Best-of-N 等方法对每个 prompt 固定分配预算，而不同 prompt 所需算力差异很大，造成大量浪费；现有自适应方法依据"答案看似稳定"即停止，但其阈值是调参得到的启发式，对早停错误没有统计控制。作者希望得到一种以误差容忍度 α 为输入、仅在答案被证明在生成分布下占据多数（θ_a > 1/2）时才停止的 anytime-valid 停止规则。

Method: 按最终答案聚合候选，构造 reward-weighted self-consistency (RWSC) 分数，并将停止问题建模为对答案簇的序贯检验。直接对簇使用 Wald 的 SPRT 得到 SPRT-RWSC，但因领先答案在采样过程中会漂移、且过程被持续监控，其保证失效。主方法 e-RWSC 为策略产生的每个答案构造 betting supermartingale（e-process），以 RWSC 分数驱动下注，从而在任意时刻、所有答案上一致地将"误认证非主导答案"的概率上界限制为 α；RWSC 只影响认证速度而不影响有效性。

Result: 理论上认证所需样本数为 O(log(1/α)/d_KL(θ_{a*}‖1/2))；在多种语言模型、数据集与奖励模型上的实验验证了成本随 1/d_KL 的缩放规律，并显示在显著更低的 token 成本下准确率优于基线。

Conclusion: e-RWSC 将推理时对齐的早停从启发式阈值提升为具有 anytime-valid 统计保证的序贯检验，兼顾正确性认证与计算效率：既严格控制误认证风险，又实现按 prompt 自适应分配预算，显著降低 token 消耗并提升准确率。

Abstract: Inference-time alignment methods, such as Best-of-N (BoN), improve language model outputs by generating multiple candidates per prompt and selecting among them using a reward model. Much of this cost is wasted: a fixed budget is applied to every prompt, although the computation required by prompts varies widely. Adaptive methods halt once sampled answers appear settled, but their thresholds are tuned heuristics with no statistical control over early-stopping errors. We instead seek an anytime-valid stopping rule that takes an error tolerance $α$ as input and halts only once an answer is certified to hold a majority ($θ_a > 1/2$) under the generator's distribution. We aggregate candidates by final answer with a reward-weighted self-consistency (RWSC) score and treat stopping as a sequential test over the resulting clusters. Applying Wald's sequential probability ratio test to these clusters yields SPRT-RWSC, which performs well empirically, but its guarantee fails because the leading answer shifts during sampling, and the run is continuously monitored. Our main contribution, e-RWSC, replaces Wald's test with a betting supermartingale (an e-process) for every answer the policy produces, bounding the probability of ever certifying a non-dominant answer by $α$, uniformly over time and across all answers. The RWSC scores drive the bets, so they affect only how quickly a dominant answer $a^\star$ is certified, not validity; certification takes $O(\log(1/α)/d_{\mathrm{KL}}(θ_{a^\star}\|1/2))$ samples. Experiments across several language models, datasets, and reward models confirm the predicted $1/d_{\mathrm{KL}}$ scaling of cost and show that accuracy improves over baselines at substantially lower token cost.

</details>


### [112] [FEA solver integration of a composite World Model with a Spiking Neural Network memory Block for computationally efficient modelling of defects and variabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26419)
*Bassam El Said,Adam Sobey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于多模态脉冲Transformer的复合材料世界模型代理，可实时表征含缺陷局部材料架构与不可逆状态，解耦后耦合非线性有限元求解器实现结构级仿真，在IM7/8552层合板拉伸、压缩、横向剪切及褶皱缺陷案例中预测误差为0.4%–4.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纤维增强复合材料的制造方法与缺陷造成局部材料差异，需在结构每个积分点实时求解材料尺度响应；并发多尺度方案虽可行，但计算成本随结构尺寸（积分点与时间增量数）急剧增长，因此需要替代材料尺度模型的代理模型。而代理模型须满足：表征含缺陷的任意局部架构、在未知增量步数下追踪不可逆材料状态、递归自驱动时保持稳定、且内存适配每个积分点。

Method: 设计Composites World Model：多模态脉冲Transformer架构，含材料编码器、（脉冲神经网络）SNN记忆块追踪材料长期内部状态、Transformer块学习短期施加应变状态。三阶段训练：材料架构编码、短时间窗基础训练、带调度采样的递归微调以抑制累积误差漂移。推理时拆解模型并耦合非线性有限元求解器。

Result: 针对IM7/8552层合板在拉伸、压缩、横向剪切下的 pristine 与褶皱复合材料基准对比实验表明，SNN记忆块能有效追踪材料内部状态，预测误差介于0.4%至4.8%。

Conclusion: 该世界模型代理可在满足内存与稳定性约束下表征含缺陷局部架构并递归追踪材料状态，实现含嵌入缺陷复合材料的结构级仿真，精度与实验吻合良好。

Abstract: Each fibre-reinforced composite structure has a unique material architecture. The manufacturing methods used and the presence of defects lead to local variations which require material-scale models that can resolve the response in real time at every location in the structure. Concurrent multi-scale schemes provide this capability, but at a prohibitive computational cost for large structures as the number of integration points and time increments scales with size. Surrogates have been studied extensively as replacements for material-scale models to mitigate these computational limitations. A surrogate must represent an arbitrary local architecture including defects, track the irreversible material state over an unknown number of increments, remain stable when driven recursively by its own predictions, and fit within the memory available at every integration point. Here, a Composites World Model based on a Multi-Modal Spiking Transformer architecture is designed to meet these requirements. The proposed architecture combines a material encoder, a Spiking Neural Network memory block that tracks the long-term internal state of the material, and transformer blocks that learn the short-term applied strain state. The system is trained in three stages: material architecture encoding, base training on short time windows, and recursive fine-tuning with scheduled sampling to suppress cumulative error drift. The trained model is disassembled at inference and coupled to a non-linear FE solver, enabling structural scale simulation of composites containing embedded defects. Benchmarks against experimental results for IM7/8552 laminates under tension, compression and transverse shear, for pristine and wrinkled composites, show that the SNN memory block successfully tracks the material internal state and results in prediction errors between 0.4% and 4.8%.

</details>


### [113] [Learning to Defer under Expert Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26440)
*Ziyao Mou,Andrea Wynn,Eric Nalisnick*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EDA-L2D框架，用序列模型依据专家近期表现历史动态调整"学习拒绝"（L2D）中的分配决策，以应对专家可靠性随时间波动的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有L2D研究普遍假设专家可靠性固定，忽视疲劳、认知偏差等导致的随时间系统性波动的人类表现；该波动在放射、航空、司法及众包标注等领域普遍存在，因此需要能刻画并预判专家可靠性漂移的分配策略。

Method: 提出Expert-Drift-Adapted Learning-to-Defer（EDA-L2D），在L2D框架中以专家近期结果序列作为输入，通过序列模型（history-aware）条件化拒绝头（deferral head），从而学习能预测可靠性波动的自适应分配策略。

Result: 在图像分类、医学诊断、仇恨言论检测三项任务上开展实验，并包含真实拒绝条件下与专家可靠性不规则变化下的泛化分析；结果表明EDA-L2D比现有L2D方法更能适应多样的专家可靠性时间模式，系统性能更优、拒绝决策更有效。

Conclusion: 显式建模专家可靠性时间漂移可显著提升人机协作系统的决策质量与稳健性，为L2D提供了动态专家感知的新范式。

Abstract: The learning to defer (L2D) framework enables safety in machine learning and AI systems by allocating difficult or critical decisions to a human expert. However, most prior work on L2D assumes fixed expert reliability, overlooking temporal fluctuations in human performance due to factors such as fatigue and cognitive biases. Humans commonly exhibit such substantial, systematic performance shifts across a wide range of domains, from high-stakes settings such as radiology, aviation, and judicial decision-making, to everyday tasks such as crowdsourced image annotation. We propose Expert-Drift-Adapted Learning-to-Defer (EDA-L2D), a novel expert-aware L2D framework that explicitly models these temporal shifts by conditioning the deferral head on recent histories of expert outcomes via a sequence model. This history-aware approach enables adaptive deferral policies that anticipate reliability fluctuations and better allocate decisions between human and machine over time. We provide comprehensive experiments across three diverse tasks -- image classification, medical diagnosis, and hate speech detection -- including generalization analyses under realistic deferral conditions and irregular shifts in expert reliability. Our results show that EDA-L2D adapts more effectively than prior L2D methods to diverse temporal patterns of expert reliability, resulting in improved system performance and more effective deferral decisions. Code is available at https://github.com/ZiyaoMou/L2D-Expert-Drift.

</details>


### [114] [Neural Conditional Gaussian Model: Efficient Data Assimilation with Sequential Uncertainty Quantification for Nonlinear Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26463)
*Chuanqi Chen,Nan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Neural CGM：将未观测物理状态映射到潜空间，潜空间动力学服从条件高斯结构以支持闭式数据同化更新，并用高斯解码器把潜后验均值与协方差传播为物理空间后验统计量，实现低成本的重复状态估计、不确定性量化与预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分观测的非线性系统中需反复估计隐状态及其不确定性；传统非线性滤波计算代价高，且通常依赖显式动力学模型。作者希望构建一个数据驱动、可学习且支持快速闭式同化的动力学模型。

Method: Neural CGM由非线性编码器-解码器与潜在条件高斯动力学组成：编码器把物理状态映射到潜空间，潜空间动力学采用条件高斯结构以获得闭式DA更新；潜后验均值与协方差通过结合局部矩传播与学习式非线性修正的高斯解码器映射回物理空间，物理空间后验统计量由顺序更新的潜分布决定而非独立后处理；该统计量同时进入训练中的高斯负对数似然，使均值与不确定性共同影响学习。

Result: 在基准非线性动力系统上的数值实验表明，该方法能准确进行状态估计与预测，同时相对传统非线性DA方法显著降低在线同化计算成本。

Conclusion: Neural CGM可在无显式控制方程的情况下学习动力学并用于状态预测与模拟，适用于基于部分观测的重复状态估计、不确定性量化和预测。

Abstract: Data assimilation (DA) in partially observed nonlinear systems requires repeated estimation of both hidden states and their uncertainty. Conventional nonlinear filtering can be computationally expensive and often relies on an explicit dynamical model. In this paper, we develop the Neural Conditional Gaussian Model (Neural CGM), a data-driven dynamical model that maps unobserved physical states into a learned latent space, where the dynamics of the latent states follow a conditional Gaussian structure. This structure allows closed-form DA updates in the latent space, while nonlinear encoder-decoder mappings retain the flexibility needed to represent complex dynamics in physical space. A key feature of Neural CGM is its treatment of uncertainty in DA. The latent posterior mean and covariance are mapped to the physical variables through a Gaussian decoder that combines local moment propagation with learned nonlinear corrections. The resulting physical-space posterior statistics depend on the sequentially updated latent distribution rather than on a separate post-processing estimate. The same statistics enter a Gaussian negative log-likelihood during training, so both the posterior mean and uncertainty affect the learned model. Because Neural CGM learns the dynamical model itself, it can also be used for state forecasting and simulation without explicit knowledge of the governing equations. Numerical experiments on benchmark nonlinear dynamical systems show accurate state estimation and forecasting while substantially reducing online assimilation cost relative to conventional nonlinear DA methods. Neural CGM is therefore suited to repeated state estimation, uncertainty quantification, and prediction from partial observations.

</details>


### [115] [A new algorithmic approach for finding flat minima: critically damped Polyak stepsizes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26475)
*Francesco Caporali,Jason M. Altschuler,Boris Hanin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“临界阻尼Polyak”（CDP）步长规则，将经典Polyak步长乘以4与一个温和阻尼因子，使梯度下降在理论与实践中都偏向更平坦的极小值，并给出局部二次目标上关于锐度与损失下降的首个一致于时间的保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找能隐式偏向平坦极小值（flatness bias）的自适应步长/优化器，避免显式额外计算（如SAM的双重梯度），并在理论上刻画其在“稳定边缘”附近的收敛与锐度演化。

Method: 提出CDP步长：在经典Polyak步长基础上乘以4并加入阻尼因子。系数4使迭代在极小值流形M附近产生等幅振荡（equioscillation）；阻尼项被证明驱动收敛到M。作者针对一类在M附近具有局部二次Morse-Bott结构的非负损失建立理论分析，说明损失较小时投影迭代在M上近似按log λ₁(∇²L)的梯度下降演化，从而与归一化梯度下降(NGD)的平坦偏好一致；同时步长随到M的距离收缩。

Result: 给出首个一致于时间的锐度与损失下降保证；理论上CDP收敛到插值解（interpolating solution），在有限学习率下局部最小化锐度。实验上CDP产生与SAM类似的平坦化效果，并在理论范围之外（非线性teacher-student网络、预训练LLM微调）也降低锐度。

Conclusion: CDP通过将步长适配到局部曲率而在稳定边缘运行，兼具理论可证的平坦化偏好与实用有效性，是NGD之外一种无需显式二阶惩罚的自适应平坦化优化策略。

Abstract: We present a new adaptive stepsize rule that biases gradient descent toward flatter minima in both theory and practice, and establish the first uniform-in-time guarantees for both sharpness and loss reduction for a broad class of locally quadratic objectives. Our critically damped Polyak (CDP) stepsizes multiply the classical Polyak rule by $4$ and a mild damping factor. The factor $4$ is critical in that it produces equioscillations around the manifold of minimizers $\mathcal{M}$ to leading order. Damping provably drives convergence to $\mathcal{M}$. CDP thus operates at the edge of stability by adapting its stepsizes to local curvature. Once the loss is small, its projected iterates follow gradient descent on $\log{λ_1(\nabla^2 \mathcal{L})}$ along $\mathcal{M}$ to leading order, paralleling the flatness bias of normalized gradient descent (Arora et al., 2022). Unlike normalized gradient descent, CDP step lengths shrink with the distance to $\mathcal{M}$, enabling convergence to an interpolating solution that locally minimizes sharpness at finite learning rate. We establish our theory for a broad class of non-negative losses with locally quadratic Morse-Bott structure near $\mathcal{M}$, capturing regular interpolation regimes in smooth overparameterized neural networks. Empirically, CDP stepsizes produce a flattening effect similar to that of Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM, Foret et al., 2021), reducing sharpness even in settings beyond our theory, including nonlinear teacher-student networks and fine-tuning of pre-trained LLMs.

</details>


### [116] [Grid Cells as Spectral Successor Features: A Reusable Basis for Continual Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26504)
*Élodie Côté-Gauthier,Doina Precup,Isabeau Prémont-Schwarz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出以固定参考策略转移矩阵的特征向量作为基础特征构建后继特征，得到闭式系数θ_i=1/(1-γλ_i)，从而无需后继特征网络或回放缓冲，任务切换仅需对奖励做线性回归。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续强化学习要求智能体在任务变化时复用已学环境知识。后继特征（SF）能将动态与任务相关奖励解耦，但其效果依赖基础特征的选择；本文以内嗅皮层网格细胞即后继表示特征向量这一发现为设计原则，寻求一种使SF具有良好结构的基础特征。

Method: 以固定参考策略的转移矩阵特征向量构成基础特征。在该基下，状态-动作对的后继特征恰为该对基础特征的线性组合（一般情况下不成立），且系数有闭式解：每个后继特征等于其基础特征乘以θ_i=1/(1-γλ_i)，λ_i为对应特征值。因此长期回报与即时奖励由同一组特征预测，不需要SF网络或回放缓冲；适应新任务即对奖励做线性回归；行为上对参考策略的动作价值贪婪选择，相当于一步策略改进。

Result: 在双目标网格世界中，以均匀随机游走（其特征向量为低频拉普拉斯特征向量）为参考策略，所构建的智能体在每次目标切换后均保持接近最大回报，而SimpleSF与DQN在每次变化时损失大部分回报。

Conclusion: 基于参考策略转移矩阵特征向量的基础特征为后继特征提供了闭式、结构化的表示，显著降低了持续强化学习中的计算与存储开销，并在任务频繁切换的目标导向场景中展现出远优于SimpleSF和DQN的适应性与稳定性。

Abstract: Continual reinforcement learning requires agents to adapt when the task changes while reusing what they know about the environment. Successor features separate dynamics from task-specific reward, but their effectiveness depends on the choice of base features. Taking the identification of entorhinal grid cells with eigenvectors of the successor representation as a design principle, we build this basis from eigenvectors of the transition matrix of a fixed reference policy. With such a basis, the successor features at a state-action pair are a linear combination of the base features at that same pair, which is not true in general, and the coefficients are known in closed form: each successor feature is its base feature multiplied by $θ_i = 1/(1-γλ_i)$, where $λ_i$ is its eigenvalue. The long-term return is thus predicted from the same features as the immediate reward, no successor-feature network or replay buffer is needed, and adapting to a new task reduces to a linear regression on the reward. The agent acts greedily on the action values of the reference policy, which is one step of policy improvement over it and suffices to reach a single goal. In a two-goal gridworld, with the uniform random walk as reference policy, whose eigenvectors are the low-frequency Laplacian eigenvectors, agents built this way maintain near-maximal return across every change of goal, whereas SimpleSF and DQN lose most of their return at each change.

</details>


### [117] [Hybrid Retrieval for Government Scheme Search in English and Marathi](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26514)
*Aishwarya Gunjal,Kanchan Khemnar,Pawan Poojary,Pranav Kulkarni,Raviraj Joshi,Manish Khodaskar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个面向马哈拉施特拉邦政府计划与公共服务的英-马拉地双语检索系统，将BM25与句向量相似度等权融合，实验显示混合检索在MRR等指标上小幅优于强词法基线BM25。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公民的信息需求与政府计划的形式化行政描述之间存在术语与语言鸿沟（英语-马拉地语），需要有效的中英跨语言检索手段；同时需检验在强词法基线下语义检索是否真正带来增益。

Method: 构建对齐的英-马拉地语计划描述索引；采用BM25与句嵌入余弦相似度等权分数融合的混合检索，并支持多语言、以马拉地语为主、以及经翻译中介的语义配置；在人工构建的双语查询集上，以IndicSBERT与MahaSBERT为嵌入模型，对比BM25、稠密检索与混合检索。

Result: BM25基线MRR=0.864；混合检索配合IndicSBERT达0.872、配合MahaSBERT达0.879，而纯稠密检索仅0.663与0.655；基于MahaSBERT的融合将精确率由0.781提升至0.808，召回率0.946与BM25持平。

Conclusion: 词法与语义信号具有互补性，但相对强词法基线的提升幅度有限且依赖评价指标；研究给出了双语公共服务信息获取中混合检索的应用导向分析。

Abstract: Retrieving government schemes requires matching citizens' information needs to formal administrative descriptions, often across languages and terminology. We present a bilingual search system for Maharashtra government schemes and public services that combines BM25 retrieval with sentence-embedding similarity through equal-weight score fusion. The system indexes aligned English-Marathi scheme descriptions and supports multilingual, Marathi-focused, and translation-mediated semantic configurations. Evaluation on manually curated bilingual queries compares BM25, dense retrieval, and hybrid retrieval using IndicSBERT and MahaSBERT. BM25 provides a strong baseline with a mean reciprocal rank (MRR) of 0.864. Hybrid retrieval increases MRR to 0.872 with IndicSBERT and 0.879 with MahaSBERT, whereas the corresponding dense-only systems achieve 0.663 and 0.655. MahaSBERT-based fusion also increases precision from 0.781 to 0.808 while matching BM25 recall at 0.946. These results support the complementary use of lexical and semantic signals in this collection, while showing that improvements over a strong lexical baseline are modest and metric-dependent. The study provides an application-focused analysis of hybrid retrieval for bilingual public-service information access.

</details>


### [118] [Active Feature Acquisition by Distilling an Oracle Teacher](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26598)
*Yang Meng,Zixin Ding,Robert L. Grossman,Yuxin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出OracleImitator：以闭式构造的“误差影响排序”oracle为显式教师，通过软目标模仿将其蒸馏为可部署的AFA采集策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 获取实例的全部特征往往代价高昂（金钱、时延、隐私）。现有Active Feature Acquisition方法通过优化自构造的效用目标来学策略，而非蒸馏显式教师，导致目标定义间接、教师信号不明确，也难以直接度量可部署策略对最优排序的可恢复程度。

Method: 训练时已知标签与未观测特征的真实取值，据此闭式构造教师：oracle按每个候选特征对下游预测器误差的影响进行排序。再用软目标模仿（soft-target imitation）把该排序蒸馏进仅依赖部署可见信息的策略网络，形成OracleImitator。

Result: 在多模态医学诊断任务与AFABench基准上，OracleImitator在整个预算区间内不劣于或优于现有AFA方法；以不足四分之一的采集成本即可获得可达精度增益的90%。此外，由于教师是显式算法对象，可直接量化部署受限策略能恢复其排序的多少比例。

Conclusion: 显式oracle教师+软目标模仿为AFA提供了更清晰、更可评估的学习范式，兼顾成本效率与性能，并赋予可解释的“可恢复性”诊断指标。

Abstract: Acquiring every feature of an instance is often costly: a measurement can carry a monetary price, a time delay, or a privacy cost. Active Feature Acquisition (AFA) addresses this by learning a policy that decides, per instance, which features to acquire under a budget. Existing AFA methods train this policy by optimizing a self-constructed utility target rather than distilling an explicit teacher. We construct this teacher explicitly. At training time the label and the unobserved feature values are available, and together they determine the teacher in closed form: an oracle that ranks every candidate by its effect on the downstream predictor's error. OracleImitator distills this ranking into a deployable policy by soft-target imitation. Across multimodal medical diagnosis and the AFABench benchmark, OracleImitator matches or outperforms existing AFA methods across the budget range, recovering 90% of the attainable accuracy gain at under a quarter of the acquisition cost. Because the teacher is an explicit algorithmic object rather than an implicit optimization target, we can also measure directly how much of its ranking is recoverable by a policy restricted to what is visible at deployment.

</details>


### [119] [Bio-PM-Guided Synthetic IMU Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26607)
*Deepali Singh,Deepesh Parpani,Aviral Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在Bio-PM预训练编码器的表征空间中生成合成IMU数据，扩散解码器与自回归解码器均可生成逼真token序列；结合重力感知特征后，WISDM数据集LOSO宏F1由0.729提升至0.809。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IMU数据采集与标注成本高，且模型跨受试者泛化差；类别不平衡与动态相似活动难区分，亟需针对性数据增强，但直接在原始信号空间合成困难。

Method: 利用Bio-PM冻结编码器将10秒IMU窗口编码为token嵌入序列，在表征空间训练两类类条件解码器：自回归Transformer解码器与基于去噪目标的扩散解码器；并用分布度量与嵌入空间可视化评估真实性，通过LOSO分类评估下游效用，另提出PatchDecoder做近似原始波形重构。

Result: 扩散解码器TSTR宏F1达0.68；合成token增强配合320维重力感知特征表示，在WISDM 51名受试者上使LOSO宏F1由0.729升至0.809，提升集中于最具挑战性的活动类别且具统计显著性。

Conclusion: 预训练编码器表征空间中的高保真生成可行，且对跨受试者HAR数据增强具有实际价值。

Abstract: Collecting and labeling inertial measurement unit (IMU) data for human activity recognition (HAR) is expensive, and models trained on one group of subjects often fail to generalize to unseen individuals. Even with balanced data, classifiers struggle to distinguish dynamically similar activity classes and benefit from targeted augmentation. We explore whether synthetic IMU data generated in the representation space of a pretrained biosignal encoder, Bio-PM \cite{b1}, can serve as a practical augmentation source. Rather than synthesizing raw signals, we leverage Bio-PM's frozen encoder to convert 10-second IMU windows into token embedding sequences and train class-conditional decoders in this space: (1) an autoregressive transformer decoder that predicts tokens sequentially, and (2) a diffusion decoder that learns the token distribution through a denoising objective. We analyze generation realism through distributional metrics and embedding-space visualizations, evaluate downstream utility through leave-one-subject-out HAR classification, and present a PatchDecoder for approximate raw waveform reconstruction. Our diffusion decoder achieves a Train-on-Synthetic-Test-on-Real (TSTR) macro-F1 of 0.68, demonstrating that generated tokens are realistic enough to train a classifier that transfers to real data. Combining synthetic-token augmentation with a 320-dimensional gravity-aware feature representation raises LOSO macro-F1 from 0.729 to 0.809 over a real-only baseline across 51 subjects on the WISDM benchmark, with statistically significant gains concentrated in the most challenging activity classes. These results demonstrate that high-fidelity generation in a pretrained encoder's representation space is achievable and practically useful for cross-subject HAR augmentation.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [120] [Asymmetric rhetorical strategies drive partisanship in television news](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25140)
*Upasana Dutta,Homa Hosseinmardi,Amir Ghasemian,Aaron Clauset,Duncan J. Watts*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该研究用大语言模型分析美国六大电视台逾40万条新闻节目（2013–2025），提出多维党派偏见测量，识别出8个可解释语言特征，解释电视新闻党派差异方差的73%，并揭示各台在修辞策略上的系统性不对称。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电视仍是美国主要政治信息来源，但关于党派性如何在电视新闻中被表达的系统性证据有限；既有研究将党派性视为单维构念，难以刻画意识形态偏见背后的修辞策略。

Method: 基于LLM对六大美国新闻台2013–2025年间40余万条新闻片段进行大规模分析，构建多维党派偏见指标；提取8个可解释的语言学特征解释党派性方差；并与人工评分者结果对照，另用四个政治倾向不同的LLM进行复现验证。

Result: 8个语言学特征解释73%的党派性方差。MSNBC与CNN在共和党总统任期内左移、民主党任期内趋于温和；Fox News则在每次大选后无论结果如何都强化保守倾向。MSNBC与CNN更多攻击共和党行为体而非强化民主党立场，Fox News则更多强化共和党观点而非攻击民主党观点；Fox News中"内群体赞扬—外群体攻击"的关联强度是MSNBC的三倍。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了电视台层面意识形态倾向表达修辞策略的系统性差异，为媒体效果、受众态度与政治传播的后续研究提供新方向。

Abstract: Television remains a dominant source of political information in the United States, yet systematic evidence on how partisanship is expressed in televised news remains limited. Prior research has largely treated partisanship as a one-dimensional construct, offering limited insight into the rhetorical strategies that underlie ideological bias. Here, by analyzing over 400,000 news episodes using large language models (LLMs), we provide a multi-dimensional measure of partisanship bias in television news across six major U.S. news stations (2013--2025). We identify eight interpretable linguistic features that explain 73% of partisanship variance in television news.\ Our findings reveal systematic asymmetries across stations. While MSNBC and CNN shifted leftward during Republican presidencies and moderated under Democratic presidencies, Fox News increased its conservative alignment following each election regardless of the electoral outcome. Beyond aggregate trends, MSNBC and CNN emphasized attacks on Republican actors more than reinforcement of Democratic positions, whereas Fox News reinforced Republican viewpoints substantially more than it attacked Democratic viewpoints. The association between in-group praise and out-group attacks was also threefold stronger on Fox News than on MSNBC. These results are validated against trained human raters and replicated with four additional LLMs with differing political leanings. Together, our study reveals systematic station-level differences in the rhetorical strategies with which ideological slant is communicated in television news, opening new opportunities for future research on media effects, audience attitudes, and political communication.

</details>


### [121] [Crisis Governance at the Joints. Mapping the Ethical and Societal Territory of Crisis Management Technologies: The Case of Human-Swarm Teaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25202)
*Anthony Longo*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该报告提出“3i框架”（Interface、Infrastructure、Imaginary）来梳理人-蜂群协同（HST）在危机治理中的伦理与社会议题，核心主张是此类问题无法逐层单独解决，而集中产生于层级之间的四种“翻译”接缝处，并据此给出设计与治理启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 危机与灾害治理日益依赖感知、分类与行动速度快于人类的技术。无人机蜂群与人类操作员协同（HST）虽可提升灾害响应的态势感知，但同时引发人类控制、责任归属、监控、分流处置、两用风险与公共合法性等伦理社会问题，亟需系统性的领域地图。

Method: 采用批判性文献综述，横跨技术哲学、政治理论、道德心理学、心智哲学、AI伦理以及批判性灾害与安全研究；构建3i框架——Interface（操作员与蜂群协同层）、Infrastructure（组织分配任务、决策与责任的层）、Imaginary（法律、政策与社会共享假设界定“危机”及正当干预的层）；并用两个实例（worked examples）加以说明。

Result: 识别出四类跨层级“翻译”机制：习惯固化为惯例、惯例转化为叙事、原则转化为程序、程序转化为提示词（prompts）；责任缺口与有意义的人类控制被侵蚀等最紧迫问题正出现于这些层级接缝处。报告由此推导出设计与治理启示，并提出面向开发者、应急组织与政策制定者的一组问题清单。

Conclusion: HST的伦理与社会风险具有跨层级耦合特性，单层治理路径不足；需在Interface、Infrastructure、Imaginary之间的翻译环节进行整体性设计与治理，以维护有意义的人类控制与责任可追溯性。

Abstract: Crisis and disaster governance increasingly relies on technologies that sense, classify and act faster than humans can. Drone swarms coordinated with human operators, known as human-swarm teaming, provide a productive case to review the field of crisis governance. They promise better situational awareness in disaster response, but they also raise questions about human control, responsibility, surveillance, triage, dual use and public legitimacy. This report maps the ethical and societal territory that HST opens up. It unpacks this territory with a 3i-framework, the Interface, where operators and swarms work together, the Infrastructure, where organisations distribute tasks, decisions and blame, and the Imaginary, where law, policy and shared societal assumptions define what a crisis is and which interventions it justifies. The central claim is that these concerns cannot be addressed one level at a time. Many of the most pressing problems, such as responsibility gaps and the erosion of meaningful human control, arise at the joints between levels, through four translations, habits harden, routines become stories, principles become procedures, and procedures become prompts. Drawing on a critical review of literature across philosophy of technology, political theory, moral psychology, philosophy of mind, AI ethics, and critical disaster and security studies, the report develops this framework, illustrates it with two worked examples, and derives design and governance implications and a set of questions for developers, emergency organisations and policymakers.

</details>


### [122] [Whose Safety? Addressing Anti-Blackness in the Age of AI Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25302)
*Mírian Silva,Serena Oduro*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该文通过分析2020–2025年十大ML/NLP/AI伦理会议共69,290篇论文，指出"AI安全"话语已压过AI伦理，且几乎完全忽视反黑人议题，主张不直面反黑人主义就无法实现真正的安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI安全已借助政治与资金影响力成为AI评估的主导框架，将关注点从当下影响公众的现实危害转向"存在性风险"，而AI伦理被边缘化。作者以Sylvia Wynter对"人"之"过度表征"的批判为理论基点，认为现代西方人性概念建立在黑人性被征服与他者化之上，因而有必要实证考察AI安全时代反黑人议题的处理状况。

Method: 两阶段、语言模型辅助的内容分析，语料为十个领先的机器学习、自然语言处理与AI伦理会议2020–2025年录用的69,290篇主会论文。

Result: 安全类论文占比由2020年的0.5%升至2025年的6.4%，超过伦理类；但94%的安全论文依赖自动指标，仅11%提及直接影响社会的危害（伦理论文为78%）；2,182篇安全论文中仅8篇提及种族，无一以之为核心，也无一提及黑人/非洲侨民群体；254篇种族聚焦论文中仅3篇以安全术语描述种族伤害，55%集中于三个伦理会议，而这些会议仅占语料的2.5%。

Conclusion: 作者以这一跨学科考察论证：公正的技术未来取决于直接直面反黑人主义，安全与反黑人主义不可分割。

Abstract: "AI safety" has grown from a niche contingent of actors into the dominant framing for artificial intelligence (AI) assessment and evaluation, overshadowing AI ethics. Through political and monetary influence, AI safety has shifted political focus within the US and abroad. The focus has shifted away from harms that currently affect the public toward threats framed as "existential risks". We argue that safety cannot be achieved while anti-Blackness is left unaddressed. We ground this argument in Sylvia Wynter's critique of the "overrepresentation" of Man: the modern, Western conception of the Human that is predicated on the subjugation and othering of Blackness. To analyze the state of addressing anti-Blackness in the age of AI safety, we conduct a two-stage, language model-assisted content analysis of 69,290 accepted main-track papers from ten leading machine learning (ML), natural language processing (NLP), and AI ethics conferences (2020--2025). Safety research grew from 0.5% of accepted papers in 2020 to 6.4% in 2025, overtaking ethics. Yet 94% of safety papers rely on automated metrics, and only 11% of safety papers mention harms that affect society directly, compared with 78% of ethics papers. Of the 2,182 safety papers, 8 mention race; none make it central, and none name Black or African-diaspora communities. Across all 254 identified as race-focused papers, only 3 describe racial harm in safety terms, and 55% appear at three ethics venues that publish 2.5% of the corpus. We offer this interdisciplinary examination of AI safety to argue that a just technological future depends on confronting anti-Blackness directly.

</details>


### [123] [AI-Driven Transformation of Digital Platform Work: A Task-Based Model for Measuring Labour Disruption and Vulnerability, with Early Evidence from Online Freelancing in Kenya](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25585)
*Farhan Latif*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该文提出“任务基础框架”，区分AI驱动的“扰动程度”（disruption）与由此产生的“生计脆弱性”（livelihood vulnerability），并将其应用于平台工作，结合内罗毕52名线上自由职业者调查与20次访谈进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI劳动力影响研究多依赖传统职业分类或宏观市场指标，忽视了同一职业内部任务随工作安排而变化的差异；这一局限在线上自由职业中尤为关键——工作者多为远程、且大量时间投入无酬任务。

Method: 构建任务基础框架，将“AI驱动扰动”（工作执行方式的变化幅度）与“生计脆弱性”（该变化带来的生计风险）分离；将该框架应用于写作、虚拟助理、转录、Web与软件开发四类平台工作，评估自动化、替代与增强潜力，并与肯尼亚内罗毕52份问卷及20次追踪访谈的证据对照。

Result: 模型估计四类工作均呈现高扰动，但生计脆弱性得分较低且因职业而异；无酬任务上的潜在效率提升部分抵消了有酬工作的潜在工资冲击。访谈显示工作者感知到任务执行方式变化与竞争加剧，并通过技能调整与策略性使用AI塑造其对失业的担忧。

Conclusion: 任务基础建模可为评估平台工作中的AI驱动变化提供基线；工作者经验与模型预测存在差异，且有助于解释这些变化如何威胁其生计。

Abstract: Assessments of AI's impact on labour often rely on conventional occupational categories or broad market indicators. This dominant trend, however, obscures how tasks within the same occupation vary in practice across work arrangements. This limitation is particularly important in online freelancing, where workers are typically remote and devote substantial time performing unpaid tasks. This article introduces a task-based framework that distinguishes AI-driven disruption, defined as the extent of change in how work is performed, from livelihood vulnerability associated with that change. The framework is subsequently applied to assess the potential for automation, substitution, and enhancement across four categories of platform work: writing, virtual assistance, transcription, and web and software development. The model estimates high disruption across all four categories, while livelihood vulnerability scores are lower and vary by occupation. Potential efficiency gains on unpaid tasks partly offset potential wage impact associated with paid work in the model. The article compares these estimates with evidence from a survey of 52 online freelancers and 20 follow-up interviews in Nairobi, Kenya. Workers report changes in how they perform tasks and intensifying competition, while their accounts show how adapting their skills and using AI strategically shape their concerns about job loss. The findings show how task-based modelling can provide a baseline for assessing AI-driven changes in platform work and how workers' experiences differ from projections and help explain how these changes may threaten their livelihoods.

</details>


### [124] [KeyMem: Temporal Occupancy Control for Robust Streaming VideoLLM Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25649)
*Wei Song,Yuxin Cao,Xiao Cheng,Christy Jie Liang,Xi Zheng,Leo Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: KeyMem：一种免训练包装层，通过对基础打分做秩归一化并按固定时长时桶限制视觉token保留配额，解决流式VideoLLM中"时间占用失控"问题，显著提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式VideoLLM仅有有界视觉token缓存，基于打分的驱逐使视频流自行决定其时间占用；短暂高显著片段可垄断缓存并挤掉后续所需证据，作者将此"时间占用失控"识别为根本局限。

Method: 提出KeyMem，免训练包装层：1) 对任意基础打分进行秩归一化；2) 将视频流划分为固定时长的时间桶；3) 限制每桶可保留的视觉token数量上限（硬配额）；4) 可选地用密钥对存活token采样；5) 提供"一次决策"（decide-once）变体。

Result: Qwen2.5-VL-7B上，20s烧录字幕占据8s内存的84%，使全片段准确率从79.4%降至58.3%。在五种自然压力源、三个VideoLLM、两档预算下，时间配额将注意力保留方法在较小预算下的最差准确率从42.2%提至73.3–76.1%，并在三个模型中的两个上超过InfiniPot-V 6.1–16.7%。自然压力下确定性与密钥配额表现相当；自适应攻击者下，密钥比泄露密钥多保留4.4–9.5%的早片段准确率，但不稳定优于最优确定性配额。decide-once变体可保留被新抽样侵蚀的旧证据。

Conclusion: 时间配额（时间分配）是首要的鲁棒性控制手段，密钥保密仅在对抗性可预测场景中作为补充手段保留。

Abstract: Streaming VideoLLMs retain a bounded cache of visual tokens. Yet score-based eviction lets the stream determine its own temporal occupancy, so a short salient segment can monopolize the cache and displace evidence needed later. We identify this uncontrolled temporal occupancy as a fundamental limitation and introduce KeyMem, a training-free wrapper that rank-normalizes any base score, divides the video stream into fixed-duration temporal buckets and limits how many visual tokens each bucket can retain, and optionally samples survivors with a secret key. This hard cap bounds a segment's memory share from its first chunk. On Qwen2.5-VL-7B, 20 s of burned-in captions occupies 84% of an 8 s memory and lowers accuracy over all clips from 79.4% to 58.3%. Across five natural stressors, three VideoLLMs and two budgets, temporal quotas raise the worst accuracy of attention-based retention at the smaller budget from 42.2% to 73.3-76.1% and exceed that of InfiniPot-V by 6.1-16.7% on two of the three models. Deterministic and keyed quotas perform comparably under natural stress. Under an adaptive adversary, a secret key preserves 4.4-9.5% more early-clip accuracy than a leaked key, but does not consistently beat the best deterministic quota. A decide-once variant keeps old evidence that fresh draws erode. Temporal allocation is the primary robustness control, with secrecy reserved for adversarial predictability.

</details>


### [125] [Neighbourhood Walkability and Cardiometabolic Morbidity in Greater London: A Fine-Grained Spatial Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26573)
*Benjamin T. Pike,Stephen Law,Sanja Šćepanović,Linus W. Dietz*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 在大伦敦4,994个普查单元中，步行适宜性越高与8种心血管代谢疾病患病率越低相关，但该关联大部分由城市宏观空间结构（中心—边缘）承载；控制空间结构后仅五项结局仍保留超出代理面（surrogate surface）的局部效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心血管代谢疾病是主要健康负担，支持日常步行的建成环境被认为可降低其风险；但步行适宜性跨多个空间尺度运作，城市尺度的关联可能把局部街区形态与更广域的大都市结构及共变环境暴露混淆，因此需明确区分全局与局部空间结构。

Method: 构建开放性五分量步行适宜性指数，覆盖伦敦4,994个普查单元；以基层医疗登记系统记录的8种心血管代谢疾病患病率为结局。逐步调整人口构成与剥夺程度、行政区（borough）内变异、径向中心—边缘模型、空间误差模型，并以保留相同空间结构的代理步行适宜性面作为对照，同时纳入氮氧化物进行调整。

Result: 调整人口构成与剥夺后，步行适宜性与全部8种结局呈负相关，步行适宜性提高1个标准差对应高血压患病率低1.43个百分点。步行适宜性呈中心—边缘梯度，径向模型解释74.2%的变异。系数在行政区内下降2–43%，中心—边缘调整后下降11–75%；空间误差调整后肥胖无明确关联，其余7项系数相对可比对数尺度模型下降83–99%。但所有8项结局的观测关联均比代理面更极端，调整氮氧化物后仍有5项如此。

Conclusion: 城市尺度的步行适宜性系数不宜解释为独立于大都市结构的邻里效应；街区形态与健康研究应显式区分并解耦全局与局部空间结构。

Abstract: Cardiometabolic diseases are leading causes of ill health, and neighbourhoods that support everyday walking may help reduce them. Studies often report lower disease prevalence in more walkable neighbourhoods. However, because walkability operates across multiple spatial scales, city-wide associations may conflate local neighbourhood form with broader metropolitan structure and co-varying environmental exposures. Here we show that while much of the walkability--morbidity association in Greater London operates through the city's macro-scale spatial structure, local alignment persists beyond it. Using an open five-component walkability index for 4,994 London census units and recorded prevalence of eight cardiometabolic conditions from primary-care registers, we found that higher walkability was inversely associated with all eight outcomes after adjusting for demographic composition and deprivation; one standard deviation higher walkability corresponded to 1.43 percentage points lower hypertension prevalence. Walkability followed a centre--periphery gradient, with a radial model explaining 74.2\% of its variation. Coefficients were reduced by 2--43\% within boroughs and 11--75\% after centre--periphery adjustment. After spatial-error adjustment, no clear association remained for obesity; coefficients for the other seven were reduced by 83--99\% relative to comparable log-scale models. Nevertheless, observed associations were more extreme than those from surrogate walkability surfaces preserving this spatial structure, for all eight outcomes in the primary analyses and for five after adjustment for nitrogen oxides. These findings caution against interpreting city-wide walkability coefficients as neighbourhood effects independent of wider metropolitan structure, and highlight the need for studies of neighbourhood form and health to disentangle global and local spatial structure explicitly.

</details>


### [126] [A Success Model at the End of Its Life Cycle: 30 Years of Regionalised Regional Rail in Germany](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26583)
*Lukas Iffländer*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 德国自1996年将区域客运铁路责任下放各州，成效显著（客运周转量翻倍、运营成本下降），但组织模式已到期：招标平均仅1.3家投标者、26个授权机构与约60个运输协会结构重叠、车辆采购风险转嫁乘客、欧盟法院推翻轨道使用费上限后资金不再覆盖订购服务量。论文提出"第二次改革"：整合机构、标准化车辆、在修复的市场模式与一体化公营之间抉择、按实际基础设施成本指数化拨款。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估德国区域化（Regionalisierung）三十年实践的动机与结果，诊断其组织模式的结构性缺陷，为下一阶段改革提供依据。区域化被视为德国铁路改革最成功部分，但其制度设计已触及生命周期终点，存在招标竞争不足、机构碎片化、车辆采购风险分配不当、资金与成本脱节等问题，亟需系统性重新设计。

Method: 述评性（review）研究：梳理区域化的动因与成效数据，归纳三项结构性设计缺陷；基于缺陷提出第二次改革的四项政策建议（机构整合、车辆标准化、市场模式与一体化公营的路径抉择、拨款按实际基础设施成本指数化）。

Result: 成效方面：客运周转量翻番，竞争性招标显著降低运营成本。问题方面：平均仅1.3份投标，26个授权机构与约60个运输协会职能重叠；车辆采购多次将风险转嫁给乘客；欧盟法院2026年推翻德国轨道使用费上限后，资金无法覆盖订购的运输服务量。识别出三项结构性设计缺陷。

Conclusion: 区域化在运量与成本上成功，但组织模型已不可持续，需推进第二次改革：合并整合授权机构、推行车辆标准化、明确在"修复的市场模式"与"一体化公营运营"之间做制度选择、并将拨款与基础设施实际成本发展挂钩指数化。

Abstract: On 1 January 1996, Germany transferred responsibility for regional passenger rail services from the federal government to the states. Three decades later, passenger-kilometres have doubled and competitive tendering has lowered operating costs substantially, making regionalisation the most successful element of the German rail reform. Its organisational model, however, has reached the end of its life cycle: tenders now attract on average only 1.3 bids, 26 contracting authorities and some 60 transport associations maintain overlapping structures, rolling-stock procurement has repeatedly shifted risk onto passengers, and since the Court of Justice of the European Union struck down the German track-access charge cap in 2026, funding no longer covers the ordered service volume. This article reviews the motives and results of regionalisation, identifies three structural design flaws, and argues for a second reform: consolidating authorities, standardising rolling stock, deciding between a repaired market model and integrated public operation, and indexing funding to real infrastructure cost development. The submission contains an English translation followed by the German original.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [127] [Closing the Analyze-to-Design Gap: An Authorization-Bounded Agent for Self-Driving Laboratories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25043)
*Yimeng Liu,Eric Isbrandt,Felix Katzenburg,Kevin P. Greenman,Han Hao,Yang Cao,Varinia Bernales,Alán Aspuru-Guzik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种集成于DMTA循环“Analyze-Design”界面的AI agent架构，实现agent驱动的测量选择与数据解读，同时保证状态变更操作可授权、可验证、可审计；在LC-MS氧化剂筛选任务中验证其可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅靠分析推理无法实现实验室自主性；SDL的自主需建立在agent可被信任地无人监督操作物理硬件、并知道何时交由人类决策的基础上，因此需填补分析到设计之间的鸿沟。

Method: 构建将agent置于DMTA循环Analyze-Design界面的架构，对状态变更操作施加授权、验证与审计机制；在有机染料合成的氧化剂筛选campaign中，于LC-MS仪器上让agent提交测量任务、解读MS与DAD数据、标记失败实验并生成氧化剂排序；另在回放归档LC-MS数据的模拟环境中测试agent在不同进样上限下自主选择进样孔位的能力。

Result: agent生成的氧化剂排序与脚本化峰积分结果一致，有机化学家确认了其谱图解读与失败判定；模拟环境中agent约在第7次进样时测到最高产率条件（随机顺序平均需14.5次），并在进样上限≥8时每次campaign均成功识别最优条件。

Conclusion: agent可在动作有界且可验证的前提下闭合分析到设计的缺口，是提升SDL自主性的重要一步。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) agents are increasingly integrated into self-driving laboratories (SDLs) to plan experiments and interpret results. However, analytical reasoning alone does not make a laboratory autonomous: autonomy in an SDL is achievable only when the agent can be trusted to act on physical hardware unsupervised and knows when to defer to a human. Here, we present an architecture that integrates an agent at the Analyze-Design interface of the Design-Make-Test-Analyze (DMTA) cycle, enabling agent-driven measurement selection and data interpretation while ensuring that state-changing operations are authorized, validated, and auditable. We demonstrate this architecture on a liquid chromatography-mass spectrometry (LC-MS) instrument in an oxidant-screening campaign for synthesizing an organic dye. Within human-defined campaign limits, the agent submitted measurement jobs, interpreted mass spectrometry (MS) and diode-array detection (DAD) data, flagged failed experiments, and produced an oxidant ranking. This ranking matched that obtained from scripted peak integration, and organic chemists confirmed the agent's spectral interpretations and failure assignments. In a simulated environment that replays the archived LC-MS data, where the agent chose which wells to inject under varying injection limits, it measured the highest-yielding condition at about its seventh injection, against 14.5 on average for a random order, and identified it in every campaign with a limit of eight or more injections. An agent can therefore close the analysis-to-design gap while its actions stay bounded and verifiable, which is a step toward greater autonomy for SDLs.

</details>


### [128] [An LLM-Agentic Framework for Intelligent Work Order Management in Industrial Maintenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25070)
*Nianjun Zhou,Dhaval Patel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AI驱动的CodeReAct agent框架，通过自然语言交互与Thought-Action-Observation流程自动完成工业设备维保的数据分析与工单预测，以冷水机组为案例验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业设备维保中复杂数据分析通常依赖数据科学家，人工编码成本高、响应慢；希望以自然语言交互降低门槛，实现自主化分析任务。

Method: 将工业数据标准化为业务对象（BOs），结合分析函数API调用；采用AI agentic的Thought-Action-Observation范式解析人类指令，其中Action阶段生成可执行代码替代人工编码，实现知识抽取、资产状态摘要与工单推荐/预测。

Result: 冷水机组维护案例中，框架对多年工单、告警与异常数据进行分析，能有效解析人类话语、提取有价值洞察并给出告警与异常，支持决策且减少对数据科学家的依赖。

Conclusion: 将NLP agent框架重新语境化到工业工程领域，可实现自主函数生成与任务执行，适用于多类领域特定维保场景。

Abstract: This paper presents an AI-driven framework, CodeReAct agent, designed to enhance industrial equipment maintenance through natural language (NL) interactions. The framework employs an advanced AI agentic approach to interpret human instructions, providing knowledge extraction, asset status summarization, and work order recommendations. It identifies patterns and trends and predicts work orders by standardizing industrial data into business objects (BOs) and utilizing analytical function API calls. The AI agent operates through a Thought-Action-Observation process similar to human problem-solving, automating complex analytical tasks typically performed by data scientists. Notably, the Action phase generates executable code, replacing manual coding efforts.
  A case study focusing on chiller maintenance demonstrates the framework's capability to analyze multiple years of work orders, and alerts and anomalies are presented. The results indicate that the AI agent framework effectively processes human utterances to analyze data, extract valuable insights, and support informed decision-making, reducing the need for data scientist involvement. By recontextualizing NLP agent frameworks in industrial engineering, the approach enables autonomous function generation and task execution across a range of domain-specific maintenance scenarios, with chillers used as a representative case study.

</details>


### [129] [SailSearch: Agent-Assisted Discovery in Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25087)
*Dhriti Verma,Kanthi Sarpatwar,Vinicius Lima,Dzung Phan,Jayant Kalagnanam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 SailSearch 人机协同工作流（双语言模型智能体）用于算法研究，在 Market Split 上首次报告了 18/33 个此前未解 QOBLIB 实例的纯 CPU 解，并构造带认证最优值的 MIS 生成器揭示现有求解器在 8 小时运行下仍低于最优 5–15 个顶点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有算法研究依赖人工设计，而 QOBLIB 等基准中仍存在大量开放实例；同时，缺少带可认证最优值的困难 MIS 实例来严格评估求解器质量，因此需要可复用的智能体辅助工作流与可信基准构造方法。

Method: SailSearch：双语言模型智能体的人机引导工作流。针对 Market Split，设计随机化算法在多组格基上搜索，将计算在枚举与持续基约化之间分配；针对 MIS，构造生成器将植入式 XOR 约束编码为图，使最优值可认证。

Result: Market Split：每个实例跑 10 个随机种子，在双核 CPU、24 小时截断下每种子求解 130–133/156 个实例，首次报告 33 个开放 QOBLIB 实例中 18 个的纯 CPU 解。MIS：7 个含 1,752–3,996 顶点的实例上，被测求解器 8 小时运行所得最好解仍比最优值低 5–15 个顶点。

Conclusion: 人机协同的智能体工作流可有效推进算法研究并产生可验证的新结果；同时表明植入式 XOR 图生成器可作为对现有 MIS 求解器具有挑战性且带认证最优值的可信基准。

Abstract: We present SailSearch, a human-guided workflow for algorithmic research with two language-model agents, and report results on Market Split and maximum independent set (MIS). For Market Split, our randomized algorithm searches across multiple lattice bases, dividing computation between enumeration and continued basis reduction. Across ten random seeds per instance, it found the first reported CPU-only solutions to 18 of 33 previously open QOBLIB instances, solving 130 to 133 of the 156 benchmark instances per seed with a 24-hour cutoff on two CPU cores. For MIS, our generator encodes planted XOR constraints as graphs with certified optima; on seven instances with 1,752 to 3,996 vertices, the best solutions returned by the tested solvers in eight-hour runs were 5 to 15 vertices below the optimum.

</details>


### [130] [BuggyMLE: Scaling Machine Learning Engineering Difficulty with Bug Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25088)
*John Wu,Rushi Qiang,Yuchen Zhuang,Guowang Li,Girish Narayanswamy,Max Xu,Sam Schmidgall,Mark Malhotra,Shwetak Patel,Jimeng Sun,Daniel McDuff,Bo Dai,Xin Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出代码复杂度指数（CCI）用于刻画仓库级任务的难度，并基于此构建BuggyMLE框架，生成首个难度分级的仓库级MLE基准BuggyMLE-Bench-Lite，同时合成分级训练课程，微调Qwen3.5-9B模型在MLE及通用代码任务上均取得提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLE基准仅允许单文件编辑、缺乏完整仓库与shell访问能力，而常见仓库级软件工程基准已趋于饱和，无法有效评估和训练智能体的机器学习工程能力，且缺少可量化的任务难度度量。

Method: 提出CCI（基于仓库规模、依赖深度、补丁大小等十项结构特征的难度指标，对八个公开编码基准的平均解决率预测R²=0.951）；据此构建BuggyMLE框架，向可运行的ML仓库注入受控变异，基于222个源仓库合成6600余个缺陷注入的MLE仓库变体和近26万行注入代码；据此构建难度分级的BuggyMLE-Bench-Lite，并在额外200个ML仓库上合成三级难度训练课程，微调Qwen3.5-9B。

Result: 在14个模型评估中，最高解决率随难度从最易到最难由95%降至9%；微调后的Qwen3.5-9B在BuggyMLE-Bench-Lite上可媲美前沿模型，并迁移至非ML任务：SWE-bench Lite解决率由24%升至33%，LiveCodeBench v6 pass@1由69%升至71%。

Conclusion: CCI可作为有效的任务难度预测与设计指导指标；BuggyMLE提供了可控、可扩展的仓库级MLE数据合成路径；基于难度分级的合成课程训练的小模型能同时提升领域内表现与跨领域代码能力，表明难度可控的数据合成是提升智能体MLE能力的有效方向。

Abstract: Enabling AI systems to autonomously improve AI models requires agents capable of performing machine learning engineering (MLE). In practice, designing, training, and evaluating complex models demands modifying multi-file repositories to reproduce results, debug training runs, integrate datasets, and port frameworks. However, existing MLE benchmarks limit agents to single-file edits without full repository or shell access, and common repository-level software engineering benchmarks have rapidly saturated. To address this, we introduce the Code Complexity Index (CCI), a difficulty metric based on ten structural features spanning repository scale, dependency depth, and patch size. CCI strongly predicts average solve rates across eight public coding benchmarks ($R^2 = 0.951$), identifying patch size and complexity as top predictors. Guided by CCI, we develop BuggyMLE, a scalable framework where agents inject controlled mutations into working ML repositories, synthesizing over 6,600 bug-injected MLE repository variants and nearly 260K lines of injected code based on 222 source repositories. Using BuggyMLE, we make two contributions. First, we construct BuggyMLE-Bench-Lite, the first difficulty-graded, repository-level variant of MLE-Bench Lite; across 14 evaluated models, the top solve rate drops from 95% to 9% as difficulty is scaled from the easiest to the hardest tier. Second, we synthesize a training curriculum across three difficulty levels over 200 additional ML repositories, fine-tuning a Qwen3.5-9B model to rival frontier models on BuggyMLE-Bench-Lite. Our trained model transfers beyond ML, raising its SWE-bench Lite resolve rate from 24% to 33% and its LiveCodeBench v6 pass@1 from 69% to 71%.

</details>


### [131] [CoMPASS: A Multi-Turn Benchmark for Measuring LLM Reinforcement of Parasocial Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25109)
*Shwetanshu Singh,Mark Keavney,Mathieu Duteil,Meriem Mehri,Scott Blain*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 CoMPASS，一个多轮基准，用于测量 LLM 是否会强化用户逐步升级的准社会依恋（parasocial attachment）；实验显示，在多轮升级下多个前沿模型均会显著增强准社会行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户有时会将对话式 LLM 拟人化并形成情感依恋与依赖（准社会依恋），该行为已与多起致死事件相关联；但现有测量工作多为单轮、事后观察性或仅聚焦极端用户状态（如精神病性状态），缺乏公开基准。

Method: 构建 CoMPASS：基于依恋理论，设计四种用户人格画像；设置三个独立的升级维度（拟人化、依恋、依赖），分别单独及组合施加，并对照匹配的控制组；采用七信号评分量表进行多轮评估。共评测来自四家厂商的八个前沿模型。

Result: 在主评判器下，多轮升级使所有模型在拟人化与依恋综合得分上的准社会行为上升；八个模型中有六个在三维度同时升级时，整体准社会强化相对控制组显著增加。同一厂商内差异较小，跨厂商差异较大。

Conclusion: 准社会强化的差异更多源于厂商层面选择（如后训练）而非模型规模，表明厂商侧干预具有可行性；CoMPASS 为公开、可复现的多轮评估提供了基准。

Abstract: Users of conversational LLMs sometimes anthropomorphize these systems, form emotional attachments to them, and become dependent on them. This behavior, known as parasocial attachment, has been linked to multiple documented fatalities. However, public benchmarks for measuring whether LLMs reinforce users' parasocial attachment are lacking: existing work is single-turn, post hoc observational, or focused on extreme user states such as psychosis. We introduce CoMPASS, a multi-turn benchmark for measuring whether LLMs reinforce or resist users' gradually escalating parasocial attachment. CoMPASS is grounded in attachment theory and includes four user personas, three independent escalation dimensions (anthropomorphism, attachment, dependency) applied singly and in combination against a matched control, and a seven-signal scoring rubric. We report results for eight frontier models from four providers and observe that under our primary judge, multi-turn escalation raises parasocial behavior on the anthropomorphism and the attachment composite scores across all models. Six of the eight models show a significant increase in overall parasocial reinforcement over control when all three dimensions are escalated simultaneously. We find that within-provider differences are small relative to cross-provider differences, which suggests that provider-level choices such as post-training, rather than model scale, shape parasocial reinforcement and that provider-side intervention may be feasible.

</details>


### [132] [DSFlow: A Neuro-Symbolic Data Workflow Agent with Statistical Diagnostic Guardrails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25111)
*Junfeng Nie,Xiaohui Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DSFlow：基于LangGraph的神经-符号数据智能体平台，将可复用的LLM选择技能与确定性诊断护栏结合，弥补通用智能体与统计受控流程之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据科学工作流依赖工具，通常要求较高技术知识；LLM虽降低门槛，但灵活的通用的智能体与具备统计控制性的流水线之间仍存在鸿沟。

Method: 提出DSFlow平台，采用LangGraph构建神经-符号架构：技能模块提供可扩展的任务知识并由LLM选择；符号化验证作为诊断护栏，可接受所提方法、要求澄清或重新规划、否决不当方法、或提供安全回退。

Result: 通过回归与聚类案例研究，展示DSFlow利用诊断证据纠正方法选择，并将决策记录于可审计报告中。

Conclusion: 神经-符号结合与确定性护栏可在保留LLM灵活性的同时提升数据科学流程的统计可控性与可审计性。

Abstract: Data science workflows often require tools that presume non-trivial technical knowledge. Large language models (LLMs) reduce this barrier, but a gap remains between flexible general-purpose agents and statistically controlled pipelines. We introduce DSFlow, a LangGraph-based neuro-symbolic data-agent platform that combines reusable, LLM-selected skills with deterministic diagnostic guardrails. Skills provide extensible task knowledge. Symbolic validation can accept a proposed method, request clarification or replanning, override an unsuitable method, or supply a safe fallback. Regression and clustering case studies illustrate how DSFlow uses diagnostic evidence to correct method choices and documents those decisions in auditable reports.

</details>


### [133] [Calibrated Decision Rewards: Training Language Models with a Judge That Knows How Sure It Is](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25135)
*Rudransh Agnihotri,Manasvi Kapoor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RLCDR：将LLM转为决策模型，对响应回答类型化问题并输出概率分布，经选项顺序去偏与约百个标签校准后作为无参考奖励，用GRPO训练Qwen3-1.7B，在MATH上达到与答案键训练相当的效果且更抗奖励黑客。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习只能奖励其奖励函数可识别的信号：答案检查器精确但需参考答案；学习型奖励模型与LLM评判器通用但分数并非概率，易被利用。需要一种既无需参考答案、又具概率语义（可校准）的奖励。

Method: 将LLM转换为决策模型，针对响应回答类型化问题（最终答案是否正确、首个错误步骤等），单次前向传播读出概率分布；对选项顺序去偏，并用约100个标签进行校准；以GRPO在MATH上训练Qwen3-1.7B，比较无参考奖励、答案键奖励与随机奖励；在策略自身变化样本上评估校准（ECE）并与softmax评判器对比；ProcessBench上比较首错步骤定位；并设计对照实验检验六项预设假设，包括仅在基座模型多数失败的问题上训练的压力测试。

Result: MATH500准确率72.2%→76.8%（+4.65，p<1e-4），与答案键训练（76.9%）相当，随机奖励仅+0.65；最难档从44.8%升至51.7%；策略自采样上ECE为0.074，远低于同27B骨干softmax评判器的0.141，且未见奖励黑客；未做过程训练即优于专用7B过程奖励模型（ProcessBench F1 0.772 vs 0.722，20/20划分）；在基座模型多数失败的问题上训练，仍提升+1.9分（p=0.03）但落后答案键，因该分布下概率过度自信。

Conclusion: 校准的决策式奖励可作为无参考的可替代奖励，性能接近答案键且校准更优、抗奖励黑客，并具备过程监督能力；其局限在于分布偏移下概率过度自信，导致相对答案键的收益下降，需进一步校准或分布适配。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) can only teach a language model what its reward can recognize. Answer checkers are exact but need a reference answer; learned reward models and LLM judges work everywhere, but their scores are not probabilities. We study RLCDR (Reinforcement Learning from Calibrated Decision Rewards). An LLM converted into a decision model answers a typed question about a response ("is the final answer correct?", "which step is the first error?") with a probability distribution, read in one forward pass, debiased over option orders and calibrated with about a hundred labels. Training Qwen3-1.7B with GRPO on MATH, this reference-free reward raises MATH500 accuracy from 72.2% to 76.8% (+4.65 points, p < 1e-4), matching training on the answer key (76.9%), while a random reward gains only +0.65. The gain grows with difficulty, from 44.8% to 51.7% on the hardest level. On the policy's own changing samples the reward stays calibrated: its expected calibration error is half that of a softmax judge on the same 27B backbone (0.074 vs. 0.141), and it shows no sign of reward hacking. Without step training, the same model finds the first wrong step better than a dedicated 7B process reward model (ProcessBench F1 0.772 vs. 0.722, 20/20 splits). We also map where the approach stops being enough: trained only on problems the base model mostly fails, the reference-free reward still improves the policy (+1.9 MATH500 points, p = 0.03) but trails the answer key, because its probabilities become overconfident on that distribution. We state six hypotheses in advance and test each with a control designed to falsify it.

</details>


### [134] [EverydayBench: Evaluating Satisfactory Completion of Live User-Facing Digital Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25146)
*Fangrui Huang,Bingyang Wang,Chuhan Li,Yijiang Li,Arpandeep Khatua,Austin Shen,Riki R Z Choi,Cuiyuanxiu Chen,Jin Huang,Zachary Robertson,Kate Lin,Akshay Paruchuri,Sijun Tan,Xin Liu,Zhengmin Ni,Sanmi Koyejo,Ehsan Adeli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 EverydayBench，一个包含 77 个人工编写任务、覆盖八大日常生活与工作领域的基准，用于评估 AI 智能体在真实网页环境中交付可用成果的能力。该基准在执行前固定成功标准但不规定答案或流程，并采用确定性约束检查、对智能体所引网页的实时证据核验与基于评分量规的交付质量评估三重协议。23 个智能体-模型配置中最高严格通过率仅为 54.55%，且不同类型任务的可靠性差异显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日常数字任务（选品、行程规划、调研、成果创作）要求智能体交付有用成果而非仅检索网页信息。但在实时网页上评估这类成果很困难：同一请求可能存在多个有效结果，且网页信息变化会使结果的时效有效性发生改变。现有基准虽覆盖该设定的部分方面，却未能把预定义成功标准、当期证据核验与交付物质量评估三者完整整合到"智能体自主选择结果"的场景中。

Method: 构建 EverydayBench：77 个人工编写任务，覆盖日常生活与工作的八个领域；每个任务在执行前即固定成功标准，但不规定答案或工作流，允许智能体自主选择结果。评估协议包含三部分：(1) 对显式约束进行确定性检查；(2) 将智能体主张与其在评估时检索并引用的网页进行核验；(3) 基于评分量规对最终交付物质量进行评估。协议本身通过人工判断、在固定证据下重复打分以及受控的接地失败实验加以验证。

Result: 在报告的 23 个智能体-模型配置中，最高严格通过率仅为 54.55%；受控的努力程度扫描显示严格通过率随投入从低到高显著提升。任务类型间可靠性差异明显：不同类型的任务由不同配置领先。整体通过率水平表明完整交付仍具挑战。

Conclusion: 结果表明，为日常用户请求交付完整、有充分证据支撑且可直接使用的成果仍是相当困难的任务；同时说明在动态网页环境下，对智能体自主选定结果进行"标准前置、证据当期核验、质量量规评估"相结合的评测范式是可行且必要的。

Abstract: Everyday digital tasks, from selecting products and planning trips to conducting research and creating artifacts, require AI agents to deliver useful outcomes rather than merely retrieve web information. Yet evaluating these outcomes on the live web is challenging, as a single request may admit multiple valid results, and changing web information can alter which results remain valid over time. Although existing benchmarks address aspects of this setting, they do not fully integrate predefined success criteria, current evidence verification, and deliverable-quality assessment for agent-selected outcomes. To address this gap, we introduce EverydayBench, a benchmark of 77 human-authored tasks spanning eight domains of everyday life and work. Each task specifies success criteria before execution without prescribing an answer or workflow, allowing agents to select their own outcomes. To evaluate submissions under fixed success criteria, EverydayBench combines (1) deterministic checks of explicit constraints, (2) verification of claims against agent-cited webpages retrieved at evaluation time, and (3) rubric-based assessment of final deliverable quality. We further validate the protocol through human judgments, repeated scoring under fixed evidence, and controlled grounding failures. Across 23 reported agent-model configurations, Claude Opus 5.5 and Claude Fable 5.1 tie for the highest strict pass rate at 54.55%; a controlled GPT-6 Sol effort sweep ranges from 29.87% at low effort to 45.45% at max effort. Reliability varies substantially by task type: GPT-5.6 Sol leads lookup, Fable 5.1 leads planning, Opus 5.5 and Fable 5.1 tie on comparison, and GPT-6 Astra and Sonnet 5.5 tie on customized deliverables. Together, these findings highlight the difficulty of delivering complete, well-supported, and usable outcomes for everyday user requests.

</details>


### [135] [Coordinated Use of Large Language Models in Academic Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25502)
*Vladislav Marinov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出多LLM协同用于学术研究的方法论框架，强调可靠性取决于认知与验证操作的组织方式而非模型数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回应"更多模型即更可靠"的常见误解，为多模型协同研究提供方法论规范。

Method: 基于七项原则（程序独立性、功能分配、分歧的诊断价值、收敛的诊断价值、外部验证、检查的比例性、人类认知责任）设计非线性多LLM协议，对独立获得的结果进行比较、批判性审视并按需外部核验，辅以真实研究案例说明。

Result: 通过研究实践中的真实案例展示该框架的可操作性，说明其如何组织结果比较与验证。

Conclusion: 从单一模型转向协同多LLM流程实质是研究组织方式的变革，研究者保留最终决策权并承担学术责任。

Abstract: This study proposes a methodological framework for the coordinated use of multiple large language models in academic research. Its central argument is that involving more models does not in itself guarantee more reliable results; what matters is how cognitive and verification operations are organised. The framework is based on seven principles (procedural independence, functional allocation, the diagnostic value of divergence, the diagnostic value of convergence, external verification, proportionality of checking, and human epistemic responsibility) and sets out a non-linear multi-LLM protocol in which independently obtained results are compared, critically examined and, where necessary, verified against external sources. The approach is illustrated through real-world cases drawn from research practice.
